**A curated resource for DNA foundation models, genomic pretraining corpora, and evaluation benchmarks.**

</div>

This repository collects links and metadata for **DNA Foundation Models (DNAFMs)**, genomic pretraining corpora, and evaluation resources. We will keep it continuously updated as the field evolves.

- 🧬 **Model resources**: architectures, pretraining objectives, tokenization strategies, and downstream biological tasks.
- 📚 **Curated corpora**: representative genomic pretraining datasets and corpus categories.
- 📊 **Evaluation benchmarks**: benchmark protocols and task-specific resources.
- 🤝 **Community-driven**: found a missing model, benchmark, dataset, paper link, or correction? Feel free to open an issue or submit a pull request.

## News

**Current status (2026-11-21): 217 models, 160 pretraining corpora, and 181 benchmarks.** All dated catalogue changes are recorded in the [Detailed update history](#detailed-update-history) below.

<summary>Detailed update history</summary>

- **[2026-11-21, 09:00 +09:00 follow-up]** Reconciled the QSSM-DNA/ClinVar additions on draft PR #5 and extended the primary-source screen. The [arXiv genomics feed](https://export.arxiv.org/api/query?search_query=cat:q-bio.GN&start=0&max_results=40&sortBy=submittedDate&sortOrder=descending) still ends at a November 19 submission, while the paginated [bioRxiv November 20–21 interval](https://api.biorxiv.org/details/biorxiv/2026-11-20/2026-11-21/0/json) grew from 143 to 198 records. Targeted GitHub searches and the current DNA/genome model and dataset listings on Hugging Face exposed no additional qualifying release. Added [KronGenome F](https://huggingface.co/just-dna-seq/KronGenome-F-1Mb/tree/9acfb4dad8c281e8eac65e97f056c1c338463cb1), a released 54.6M-parameter, ten-layer KronLSTM/U-Net model with up to 1,048,576-base context, masked-base and functional-track training, and block-JEPA auxiliary learning. The CC BY-NC 4.0 release contains a 218-MB safetensors checkpoint, a 373-MB fixed-131,072-base ONNX export, configuration and channel metadata; [model code](https://github.com/antonkulaga/kron-fm/tree/8ae6fe4ab0956cd737c52ad27e013ea430501099) is AGPL-3.0. This supersedes the earlier unreleased-checkpoint status for this genome model, not every codon-model experiment in kron-fm. Counted its documented mixed-genome training recipe and model-card chr8/chr9 sequence-to-function evaluation once each. Step 96,000 is intermediate in a planned 380,000-step run; the card reports lower scores than AlphaGenome v1, and no independent numerical reproduction, complete processed corpus or frozen per-window evaluation bundle was verified. G-loop DNABERT-2 is a task-specific fine-tune, GenomeSpy's new ChromBPNet/BAT/CAT uploads are format conversions of supervised models, and `dnotitia/dna-eval` evaluates Korean general-purpose LLMs rather than nucleotide models. DNA-50M/175M still lack training provenance, and new pathomics/knowledge-graph work does not establish a new nucleotide backbone. The [Q Labs repeated-data study](https://qlabs.sh/research/applications) now provides a detailed technical article and appendix, but no public code, checkpoints, per-example outputs or archival identifier was located; it remains a follow-up candidate rather than a counted public resource. Totals: **217 models, 160 corpora, and 181 benchmarks**.

- **[2026-11-20, 09:02 +09:00 follow-up]** Reconciled the earlier same-day PlantCaduceus audit and extended the primary-source screen. The paginated [bioRxiv November 19–20 interval](https://api.biorxiv.org/details/biorxiv/2026-11-19/2026-11-20/0/json) grew from 117 to 224 records; the [arXiv genomics feed](https://export.arxiv.org/api/query?search_query=cat:q-bio.GN&start=0&max_results=30&sortBy=submittedDate&sortOrder=descending) still reaches November 18, and post-boundary GitHub/Hugging Face searches exposed no additional qualifying release. Recovered [QSSM-DNA v1.0.0](https://github.com/Gumgo91/QSSM-DNA/releases/tag/v1.0.0), a 1,391-parameter four-qubit recurrent density-matrix encoder pretrained by next-base prediction in exact classical simulation on hg38 chromosome 22. Counted its chromosome-specific pretraining recipe once and its transfer study once: ten seeds at 0.25M and selected 2.5M-token budgets, three Genomic Benchmarks tasks, a released 1,405-sequence chr9/chr10 promoter holdout, random-register/GRU/k-mer/CNN controls, fixed result tables and a checksummed 114-MB per-seed prediction archive. The cited *Physica Scripta* paper is marked in press but no DOI or publisher record was located; no pretrained checkpoint/run directory is released, and the study does not demonstrate execution on quantum hardware. Also promoted the completed reduced run of [glm-variant-pathogenicity at `bfd20dd`](https://github.com/Yasmin-maker1/glm-variant-pathogenicity/tree/bfd20ddcf99c7c6c713449b9ceaf5802d381e3c3) as one evaluation resource. It compares fine-tuned and frozen DNABERT-2, Nucleotide Transformer v2 and HyenaDNA, plus zero-shot controls for the two masked models, with CADD v1.7 on a gene-disjoint ClinVar 2026-11-15 subset of 24,000/3,000/4,000 variants. Only one seed, a fixed learning rate and at most three epochs are complete; processed data, per-run predictions, fine-tuned weights and checkpoints are not committed, five test variants lack the common CADD comparison, zero-shot coverage differs, and the planned full three-seed/learning-rate/long-context study remains pending. Neither release is counted as an additional general-purpose benchmark dataset beyond these evaluation entries. Totals: **216 models, 159 corpora, and 180 benchmarks**.

- **[2026-11-20]** Reconciled merged PR #4 and audited the [arXiv genomics index](https://arxiv.org/list/q-bio.GN/recent) through November 19, all 117 returned [bioRxiv November 19–20 records](https://api.biorxiv.org/details/biorxiv/2026-11-19/2026-11-20/0/json) using 30-record pagination, recent GitHub repositories, and ten recent DNA-named models / ten genome-named datasets on Hugging Face. Added [plant-read-placement](https://github.com/gbadedata/plant-read-placement/tree/342f1abc03d0c0a14db27ccc234c534efff290a5), an MIT evaluation of PlantCaduceus versus k-mer and minimap2 baselines on simulated Nanopore reads from 28 plant species. Verified that the committed preregistration and two configuration files match their frozen SHA-256 hashes; aggregate test results and a zero-deviation run record are public. Raw reads, trained heads/adapted weights, calibration files and the genome lock referenced by the freeze are not in the repository; no tagged release or standalone manuscript was located, and numerical reproduction was not run. This is one evaluation resource, not a new model family or pretraining corpus. SCOPE, sequencing compression, pairgenomealign, haploblock hashing, RapidGLM, fmaudit and protein/agentic studies were screened out as single-cell, compression, alignment, statistical, genotype-representation or other out-of-scope resources. No additional qualifying model or pretraining corpus was confirmed. Totals: **215 models, 158 corpora, and 178 benchmarks**.

- **[2026-11-19, 09:00 +09:00 follow-up]** Reconciled the earlier same-day [glmtrust](https://github.com/asediya/evo2-reliability-atlas/tree/174e7cf860d417f27a137fb8e0fbe87e20157a15) commit on draft PR #4 and extended the primary-source audit. The [arXiv genomics index](https://arxiv.org/list/q-bio.GN/recent) now reaches November 18, and the paginated [bioRxiv November 18–19 interval](https://api.biorxiv.org/details/biorxiv/2026-11-18/2026-11-19/0/json) grew from 202 to 253 records; recent GitHub repositories and DNA/RNA/genome-named Hugging Face models and datasets were also screened. Added [Coarse-to-Fine Genomes](https://huggingface.co/datasets/Iddah/coarse-to-fine-genomes/tree/578903a2e95d1f66f493a18d5a008c0c2c67f579) once as a pretraining resource. Its CC BY-SA 4.0 GLOBDB r232 release supplies genus-disjoint validation splits and three at-most-8,192-token representations: 22,066,393 gene-plan examples / 548,380,795 tokens, 8,363,569 protein examples / 37,940,321,141 tokens, and 3,409,992 coding-DNA-plus-intergenic-infilling examples / 21,752,467,654 tokens, plus validation sets and per-genome provenance. Recovered [DeepINSIGHT](https://github.com/Julien-Mutton/DeepINSIGHT/tree/54b7f3a16ac3380893df70328d9f9842f5575688), a fully recorded negative-result audit of whether 1,001-bp HyenaDNA embeddings or TopK sparse-autoencoder codes add information to LINSIGHT. Its MIT pipeline, checks and result files cover 1,564 usable HGMD positives in unmatched, TSS-matched and region-matched panels, a held-out-genome likelihood test and power simulations; the licensed HGMD pickles and approximately 95 GB of upstream inputs are not redistributed, the locked three-seed/10%-holdout HGMD plan differs from the current five-seed/50%-holdout defaults, the 1% run is pending, and no archival paper, tag or release was located. Also added the versioned [CoPRA open replay v2026.11.17](https://github.com/simshk2023/protein-rna-affinity-audit/releases/tag/v2026.11.17), which releases 4,512 endpoint predictions and frozen replay/figure inputs for 752 absolute-affinity units, 338 ordinary mutation pairs and 220 local mutation pairs. Its 160-check clean-room replay is public, while ProNAB observations, independent-validation measurements, third-party model weights, service response bodies and fitted PRA-Pred coefficients require the documented external routes; no public manuscript identifier was located. [ToDNA](https://github.com/ChangzhiJiang/ToDNA) currently releases Evo 2 adaptation/design code without a paper, licence, checkpoint or populated result archive; [glm-variant-pathogenicity](https://github.com/Yasmin-maker1/glm-variant-pathogenicity) explicitly reports that full experiments remain in progress. [MARRI](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.17.767455) is a downstream RNA-interaction framework whose paper-linked GitHub repository returns 404, and its separate candidate-screen dataset is neither experimentally validated nor MARRI-scored. REGAL and the admixed-population expression study are task-specific design/fine-tuning studies rather than new foundation-model families, while the new circRNA repository is a secondary-structure data archive without a frozen multi-model evaluation. None was counted as a new sequence foundation-model family. Totals: **215 models, 158 corpora, and 177 benchmarks**.

- **[2026-11-18, 09:05 +09:00 follow-up]** Reconciled the AlphaGenome 2/Uni-RNA/LAMP work on draft PR #4 and extended the primary-source audit. The [arXiv genomics index](https://arxiv.org/list/q-bio.GN/recent) still ends on November 17, while the paginated [bioRxiv November 17–18 interval](https://api.biorxiv.org/details/biorxiv/2026-11-17/2026-11-18/0/json) grew from 190 to 286 records. Recent GitHub releases/changes and DNA/RNA/genome-named Hugging Face models and datasets were also screened. Added [RNA-HeadDistill v1.0.0](https://github.com/Minton13/RNA-HeadDistill/releases/tag/v1.0.0), a fully trained RiNALMo-micro adaptation distilled from 15 selected attention heads of nine RNA/DNA teacher models over 2,114,289 sampled RiNALMo-corpus sequences. The MIT code release supplies an AGPL-3.0 844-MB student archive, checksummed teacher snapshots and a verified 2.0-MB derived-data package. Counted its functional-head atlas and downstream/ablation/system evaluation once as a benchmark: the archive contains 90,565 head-probe records across 37 models and 16 tasks, not the root README's older 84,066-record/26-model/15-task summary. No raw sequence corpus, downstream labels or 8.8-TiB teacher cache are distributed, and no public manuscript or archival citation is yet available. Recovered [MFASS v2 at its reviewed scientific revision](https://github.com/rewire-bio/rewire-benchmarks/tree/bee9133b83f3aedaf2bbb9013f1875515845607e/benchmarks/mfass): it corrects 7,770 reverse-complemented assay sequences, fixes a 19,409/8,324 exon-and-gene-grouped split, and releases complete DNABERT-2, specialist and baseline predictions with 2,000 group-bootstrap comparisons. Recovered the October [architectural-diversity study](https://www.preprints.org/manuscript/202610.0761) after its [repository was completed at `9c452fce`](https://github.com/LorisNanni/Leveraging-Architectural-Diversity-for-DNA-Sequence-Classification/tree/9c452fce196411d01e1e7af6428dde6524b5c3cd): four architectures under four training configurations, fixed viral-noise and Anellovirus OOD tests, and three seed-specific homology-clustered ECO/ENH splits. Its five-part RAR release has no repository licence, tag, checkpoint, prediction or committed result table. Retained [LAMP](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.13.766567) as a paper-described diagnostic benchmark whose announced public tool, exact splits and numerical reproduction remain unverified. The newly posted BPNet motif-cooperativity study is a supervised biological analysis rather than a foundation-model benchmark, and the embedding-kernel study spans sequence-function regression without a GFM-specific release. KmerFormer now has public task-model weights but remains a short-read taxonomy classifier; DNA-50M/175M and `just-dna-seq` Hub updates remain metadata/Parquet refreshes without new training or evaluation provenance. No additional standalone pretraining corpus qualified. Counts: **215 models, 157 corpora, and 174 benchmarks**.

- **[2026-11-17]** Reconciled the DeepRNAi follow-up (`3cc4625`), screened the [arXiv genomics index](https://arxiv.org/list/q-bio.GN/recent) through November 16, all 193 returned [bioRxiv November 16–17 records](https://api.biorxiv.org/details/biorxiv/2026-11-16/2026-11-17/0/json) using 30-record pagination, recent GitHub repository searches, and ten recent DNA-named Hugging Face model records. Added the [apicomplexan-specific GPN study](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.12.766050) from its official abstract: masked-nucleotide pretraining on 455 assemblies from 76 species and held-out-chromosome regulatory evaluation in P. falciparum. Full-text XML returned HTTP 429; exact model size, processed splits, weights and code remain unverified. Added [B-RANKO](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.12.766109), verified its [repository at 5e31cb6](https://github.com/filipvucenik/B-RANKO/tree/5e31cb648ea186ea46daf612a7f5154e9d57c04c) and two open CC BY 4.0 [Zenodo checkpoints](https://zenodo.org/records/21242783). Its RNAcentral 26.0/Rfam 15.1/GARNET source recipe is public, but the YAML keys do not match the preparation script (for example, `datasets` versus `rna_datasets`); do not describe it as runnable without repair or as a frozen processed corpus. Its generation evaluation is distinct from reused BEACON/AptaDiff data. Refreshed [Vir2vec/vGUE](https://github.com/pabloarozarena/Vir2vec/tree/aa027eb10ea90c4afc5c53aa1f2b63652137fbb2): the old simoRancati repository returns 404, the new code is MIT, the Hub model is still manually gated, and seven public Parquet task subsets are pinned at `ae59bf5`. Hub subsets are named `test`, while the classifier notebook performs nested 5-by-3 stratified cross-validation; these are not predefined classifier train/test splits. Dataset-card narrative counts differ from its structured metadata, so no harmonized sample total is asserted. The 09:04 +09:00 follow-up completed the pending [AlphaGenome 2 paper and asset audit](https://storage.googleapis.com/deepmind-media/DeepMind.com/Blog/alphagenome-2-deciphering-how-cells-read-dna-to-better-understand-disease/alphagenome-2.pdf). Its evaluation suite uses held-out 1-Mb genomic intervals, a chromosome-level variant split, temporal CELLxGENE holdouts, 184 held-out bulk tracks, GTEx/TenK10K/IBDverse QTLs, CRISPRi enhancer-gene screens and GWAS prioritization; the paper provides source/accession mappings and filtered prediction atlases, while complete evaluation code, a frozen benchmark package, per-example results, model source and weights remain unavailable. Counted this as one paper-defined benchmark, not a new corpus. Refreshed the existing [Uni-RNA](https://github.com/ComDec/unirna_tf/tree/d4f26510af1034ef132b9b45df4737cfb6092f61) entry with its official manually gated [22.68-GB Hub release at `590b7ce4`](https://huggingface.co/pkugrc/Uni-RNA/tree/590b7ce464d5d82efa0bb09502e5a32a3bb9b11b), which contains 85/286/676-MB L8/L12/L16 base weights plus task files, features and downstream checkpoints under CC BY-NC 4.0; this is a release-status refresh rather than a new model or benchmark count. The post-boundary arXiv list remained unchanged, 305 Crossref registrations and targeted GitHub/Hub searches exposed no further qualifying nucleotide foundation resource. The new cross-species MIC repository lacks a public manuscript, data, results and licence and evaluates conventional whole-genome AMR predictors; DNA-50M/175M again received metadata-only refreshes. S1Pert, EAGLE-MUT, OrthoSimulator, AptaRender, spatial/cell-state, protein, and agentic candidates were likewise not added. Counts: **214 models, 157 corpora, and 170 benchmarks**.

- **[2026-11-16, 09:12 +09:00 follow-up]** Reconciled the pre-existing AlphaGenome 2/seqLens update, then checked the unchanged [arXiv genomics index](https://arxiv.org/list/q-bio.GN/recent) through November 13, the current [November 15-16 bioRxiv interval](https://api.biorxiv.org/details/biorxiv/2026-11-15/2026-11-16/0/json), all 22 Crossref registrations for prefix `10.64898` created on November 15-16, authenticated GitHub repository/change searches, and recent public Hugging Face DNA/RNA metadata. Promoted the previously deferred [DeepRNAi benchmark at revision `7b5f8223`](https://github.com/deepgenomics/DeepRNAi/tree/7b5f82203c40566eb0abcb2b59c08074e2044599) after its designated repository became public. The CC BY 4.0 release contains 1,844,382 measured siRNA-readout-transcript pairs from 240 siRNAs, with fixed 164/76-siRNA train/test splits (1,286,558/557,824 rows), predictions from mini-DeepRNAi with and without biological-foundation-model embeddings, partial random-forest predictions, and a notebook evaluating on-target Pearson correlation plus near-perfect-match and seed-match off-target average precision with 2,000 bootstrap replicates. This is counted as one supervised evaluation resource, not as a foundation-model family or pretraining corpus: the release omits the full 16-million-pair DeepRNAi training collection, transcript sequences/BioFM embeddings, model weights, and training/inference code, and has no tag or GitHub release. The only new bioRxiv interval record was unrelated neuroscience, no new arXiv genomics entry appeared, and no additional independent model family or pretraining resource qualified. Catalogue totals: **212 models, 155 corpora, and 167 benchmarks**.

The paper reports 1,354 balanced genomes whereas the repository says 1,355; retain this discrepancy rather than silently harmonizing it. - **[2026-11-15, 09:12 +09:00 follow-up]** Extended the pre-existing LGMD/BenchmarkingLLM pass across the unchanged [arXiv genomics listing](https://arxiv.org/list/q-bio.GN/recent) through November 13, the current 30-record [November 14-15 bioRxiv response](https://api.biorxiv.org/details/biorxiv/2026-11-14/2026-11-15/0/json), all 34 Crossref registrations for prefix `10.64898` created on November 14-15, authenticated GitHub repository/change searches, and the latest accessible DNA-named Hugging Face model metadata. Its public FTP mirror exposes checksummed task files plus the 155M-parameter human and seven-genome BERT-series checkpoints, but the repository has no licence, tag, or release; the newly documented matched-random-weight directory is absent from the published checksum manifest and was not listable on the FTP mirror. The BERT series therefore remains an experimental control rather than a separately counted foundation-model family or corpus. The designated [DeepRNAi repository](https://github.com/deepgenomics/DeepRNAi) still returns 404. No additional independent model family or pretraining resource was confirmed. - **[2026-11-15]** Reconciled the November 14 MicroDUET/ModBERT/mRNA-benchmark follow-up and checked the current [arXiv genomics listing](https://arxiv.org/list/q-bio.GN/recent) (latest date: November 13), all 67 records returned by the paginated [November 14-15 bioRxiv interval](https://api.biorxiv.org/details/biorxiv/2026-11-14/2026-11-15/0/json), recent GitHub repository searches, and the ten most recently modified DNA-named Hugging Face models. Recovered [LGMD-associated variant benchmarking v1.0.0](https://github.com/sarvezh1/lgmd-genomic-foundation-models/tree/v1.0.0), released November 7: 6,960 variants in ten genes with 4,866/2,094 fixed train/test examples, six genomic model architectures, and complementary GPN-Star/AlphaGenome scores. The release supplies canonical variants, predictions, metrics and validation code; original training/inference is only partly reproducible. Its exact-locus-disjoint split is not region-disjoint (median test-to-training distance 3 bp), and ClinVar labels do not uniformly establish LGMD diagnoses. Recovered the August 17 Genome Biology [BenchmarkingLLM study](https://doi.org/10.1186/s13059-026-04238-0), comparing NT, DNABERT, HyenaDNA and Evo 2 using ClinVar reference/alternate sequences and variant-site versus surrounding-context scores. Its [MIT code](https://github.com/Lab-CoMBINE/BenchmarkingLLM) provides preparation/inference scripts and a 100-variant example, rather than a verified complete frozen evaluation bundle; the paper links a [versioned archive](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21718081). Both additions reuse existing models and source corpora. [Reticular](https://github.com/reticular-ai/reticular-genome-tokenization) concerns regional diploid-genotype tokenization, with proprietary fitting and no released biobank model weights; it was not counted as a raw-nucleotide foundation model. The designated [DeepRNAi repository](https://github.com/deepgenomics/DeepRNAi) still returns 404. No new independent pretraining corpus was confirmed. Catalogue totals: **209 models, 152 corpora, and 164 benchmarks**.

- **[2026-11-14]** Reconciled the pre-existing same-day BryoCAD commit, then extended the primary-source audit through 09:16 +09:00 across the [arXiv genomics listing](https://arxiv.org/list/q-bio.GN/recent), all 90 records returned by the [November 13-14 bioRxiv interval](https://api.biorxiv.org/details/biorxiv/2026-11-13/2026-11-14/0/json), all 295 Crossref registrations for prefix `10.64898` created November 13-14, authenticated GitHub searches/changes, and current Hugging Face DNA/RNA model and dataset metadata. The four November 13 arXiv entries concern enzyme discovery, clinical variant review, EBV cell programs, and protein mutation effects rather than nucleotide foundation-model resources. Added [MicroDUET](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.11.765853), which adapts a 26.6M-parameter ProkBERT backbone with separate 3.5M-parameter phylogeny and function adapters. Its held-out evaluation covers cellular/viral species retrieval, gene-family retrieval, an independent antimicrobial-resistance-class task, *Mycobacterium tuberculosis* and mpox lineage/clade recognition, rifampicin resistance, and retrospective mpox anomaly detection against 17 other encoders. Added [ModBERT](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.12.766258), a soft-target masked model over probabilistic A/C/G/T/5mC inputs built from population allele frequencies and CpG methylation in 242 members of the 1000 Genomes Long-Read Sequencing Consortium. Counted that documented personal-genome pretraining resource and ModBERT's 11-histone-mark, 69-transcription-factor, held-out-locus/individual accessibility, and caQTL evaluation suite. Neither new model currently exposes public code, weights, an exact processed training snapshot, or fixed evaluation artifacts, so the rows make no software/model-licence claim. Added the [region-aware mRNA foundation-model benchmark](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.12.766253), which reproduces 380 published model-dataset-target evaluations and compares global with 5′ UTR/CDS/3′ UTR pooling for 20 frozen models from 18 families on mRNABench v1.3's ten splits. Revision [`2e009f8`](https://github.com/Sanford-Labs/rnaregionpooling/tree/2e009f8fbea323f6c184f1571d6fef6cc520c5a0) provides the full fetch/embed/probe/control/perturbation/report pipeline and locked Python environments; generated embeddings/results are excluded (the documented working footprint is 959 GB), most fetched model/data revisions are not pinned, and the repository has no licence, tag, or release. [DeepRNAi](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.12.766311) announces a 1.84M-pair benchmark from 240 siRNAs with a 164/76-siRNA train/test split, but its designated `deepgenomics/DeepRNAi` repository returned 404 and was deferred until the data are actually public. Post-boundary kron-fm work adds KronGenome ablations and further unfinished SynCodonLM snapshots without released checkpoints or a stable paper; tisiago remains a downstream AlphaGenome adaptation, and the latest DNA-50M/175M changes are automated card-refresh commits that still provide no training or evaluation provenance. Retained the earlier [BryoCAD](https://github.com/yenmr/BryoCAD/tree/717711dfd81a99f697c1dda2efc1f0f77e6ac62a) code, 17-genome manifest, and reconstruction refresh; MarinDNA's training/evaluation PRs remain open drafts. Catalogue totals: **209 models, 152 corpora, and 162 benchmarks**.

- **[2026-11-13]** Reconciled the November 12 viral-embedding follow-up and the pre-existing same-day MarinDNA commit, then extended the primary-source audit through 09:16 +09:00. The [arXiv genomics listing](https://arxiv.org/list/q-bio.GN/recent) still ends on November 11; the earlier pass covered 136 returned records in the [November 12-13 bioRxiv interval](https://api.biorxiv.org/details/biorxiv/2026-11-12/2026-11-13/0/json), and the extended pass screened all 227 Crossref registrations for prefix `10.64898` created on November 12-13, authenticated GitHub searches/changes, and current Hugging Face DNA/RNA-model and genome-dataset metadata. DeePEn and OMBB coarse-tuning concern protein models, while genomic-fm-lab is a small synthetic-data teaching experiment. Verified the newly public [exp593 research archive](https://huggingface.co/marin-dna/marin-dna-exp593/tree/eb26947f8cd596e5bbfd954e434855d5666b030c), including four completed BF16 exports: 216B text-start FIM, 432B DNA-start FIM, 665B FIM, and 665B AR control. The [standalone 665B FIM distribution](https://huggingface.co/plantcad/marin-dna-exp593-1.5B-60M-665B/tree/27b023bc4d38c0302f41265f05dfe24e5ba15460) declares Apache-2.0 and ships four weight shards, tokenizer/configuration files, provenance and checksum manifests. Its 665,455,079,424 cumulative DNA-training input tokens correspond to 633,229,939,080 prediction targets; these include repeated presentations and exclude original language pretraining rather than measuring unique bases. It continues the 545B intermediate exp586 checkpoint for 120B inputs, uses lowercase DNA, and requires the pinned custom inference implementation. Updated the existing model family and corrected its corpus distinction: the earlier m5.1 animal mixture differs from the 65-species PlantCAD inventory used for exp586/593. Recovered that missing versioned inventory as one documented corpus row, without claiming an independently verified standalone data download. [Training PR594](https://github.com/Open-Athena/marin-dna/pull/594) and [evaluation PR23](https://github.com/plantcad/plantcad2/pull/23) remain open drafts; released weights supersede the previous pending-weights status, but no new MarinDNA benchmark or downstream-superiority claim is added. Recovered the previously missing [MolCrawl genome family](https://github.com/mmai-framework-lab/MolCrawl/tree/c5f32119817301b22ec07914141225c945deec3a): ten public GPT-2, BERT, and DNABERT2-labelled base checkpoints from 89M to 1.49B parameters with 1,024-token context, MIT training/data code, and Apache-2.0 model cards. The cards identify GRCh38, so the existing corpus row was enriched rather than duplicated; they contain several templated architecture/parameter mislabels, the current code also targets a broader RefSeq pipeline, and no paper, exact processed snapshot, fixed biological benchmark release, tag, or GitHub release was located. Added the now-complete [AlphaGenome promoter-disruption consistency benchmark](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.11.06.764851v1): revision [`ee88c992`](https://github.com/SpielmannLab/S2F_PromoterDisruption/tree/ee88c992da61c647b13d5a36048e68c34df73381) releases the builders, scoring code, one plot, and six result tables for dinucleotide shuffling, GC-adjusted random substitution, and N-masking of 2,111 promoter-gene pairs across 20 tissues. Each perturbation provides 42,220 CAGE and 73,885 RNA-seq rows from 500-kb AlphaGenome contexts. Intermediate RDS/input tables and complete figure-analysis code are absent, stochastic builders leave the seed unset, AlphaGenome/dependency revisions are unpinned, and the repository declares no licence, tag, or release. Linked the newly registered [RNAtomy preprint](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.10.765792), which reports seven tasks; the public snapshot still omits mean-ribosome-load data, and the RNAtomy tree still lacks its README-named pretraining/top-level fine-tuning entry points, committed results, and `LICENSE`. Post-boundary kron-fm work is PPG-only while SynCodonLM pretraining remains unfinished with no public weights/results archive; tisiago remains an in-progress AlphaGenome adaptation whose source data and full result artifacts are not distributed. Catalogue totals: **207 models, 151 corpora, and 159 benchmarks**.
- **[2026-11-12]** Reconciled the FunFormer follow-up before checking the [arXiv genomics listing](https://arxiv.org/list/q-bio.GN/recent) through November 11, all 184 returned records in the [November 11-12 bioRxiv interval](https://api.biorxiv.org/details/biorxiv/2026-11-11/2026-11-12/0/json), all 193 Crossref registrations for prefix `10.64898` created on November 11-12, authenticated GitHub repository searches, and recent Hugging Face DNA-model/genome-dataset metadata. The three newest arXiv entries concern single-cell network inference, bacterial recombination estimation, and single-cell counterfactuals rather than new nucleotide foundation models. Added the new [viral-fragment embedding benchmark](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.09.765411), which compares DNABERT-2, DNABERT-S, ViraLM, and Evo2-7B with k-mer, canonical-k-mer, Jensen-Shannon, and MinHash baselines. Its [public reproduction repository at revision `e9919c9`](https://github.com/tse-kladman/glm_vectorizing/tree/e9919c9c96fd93f59842297ab4ceac0e0885fc4c) supplies the complete processing and metric pipeline, tests, the ICTV MSL40 metadata, fixed layer/k-mer selections, per-subsample results, statistical tables, and figure regeneration for 50 seeded reference draws across 39 viral classes plus held-out Egovirales and Mriyavirales. The source and held-out genome FASTAs and extracted embeddings are not distributed; transformer revisions are unpinned, the committed manifest records a dirty source tree, and the repository has no licence, release, or tag. [CheckAMG](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.09.23.753886) is a downstream auxiliary-viral-gene tool using a protein genome language model rather than an additional nucleotide-FM benchmark, while [MicroBench](https://github.com/Kirk3gaard/MicroBench) provides microbial sequencing/basecalling data rather than a documented DNA-FM evaluation protocol. Refreshed [GENEB at revision 9642d48](https://github.com/darlednik/GENEB/tree/9642d481e40c0af23995dcd162b779613f789f97): 40 models, 100 tasks, 13 functional categories, full-data/10-shot/1-shot frozen-embedding logistic regression, five seeds, and MCC as the primary metric; the complete model-specific extractors are on its `dev` branch. This is a metadata enrichment of an existing benchmark, not a new resource count. Added [BryoCAD](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.09.765485), a PlantCAD2-derived masked language-model family with bidirectional reverse-complement-equivariant Mamba2, single-nucleotide tokenization, and 8,192-bp context. Official Small and Large repositories provide weights, tokenizer, configuration, and loading code. Their model cards document continued pretraining on 17 genomes representing 17 orders across mosses, liverworts, and hornworts; the documented corpus is counted, but a standalone processed-data release and complete evaluation code/data were not located. The model cards declare no licence. The GeneCAD and ATAC LoRA derivatives are downstream adaptations, not additional model families or benchmark rows. Post-boundary checks found only chart harmonisation in AMP 2026; MarinDNA exp593 remains a draft training PR; ALBench-S2F remains unlicensed and actively expanding; kron-dna reports a 150k-step interim SynCodonLM snapshot but still labels training unfinished and releases no weights; and the refreshed DNA-50M/175M cards remain undocumented. Catalogue totals: **206 models, 150 corpora, and 158 benchmarks**.
- **[2026-11-11]** Reconciled the November 10 RNAtomy/GBST-BERT/GNE-Audit follow-up and the earlier same-day NT-probing/Sequence-Pruning update, then extended the primary-source audit through 09:00 +09:00. The [arXiv genomics listing](https://arxiv.org/list/q-bio.GN/recent) contains six November 10 entries, the earlier [November 10-11 bioRxiv interval](https://api.biorxiv.org/details/biorxiv/2026-11-10/2026-11-11/0/json) contained 104 returned records, and a later Crossref/bioRxiv registration pass, authenticated GitHub searches, and current Hugging Face DNA/RNA metadata were screened. Retained the MIT-licensed [NT layer-wise probing audit](https://github.com/tehreemraouf-06/nt-layerwise-probing/tree/d75ad57e7729f313e3ce627f267e9b2a5f2faf7f) as a four-task evaluation protocol and [Sequence Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10386) as an Apache-2.0 pretraining toolkit whose checkpoints remain promised; linked the newly posted [CPDD preprint](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.07.765118) and refreshed [mRNABench](https://doi.org/10.1101/2025.07.05.662870) to 13 datasets, 81 tasks, and 29 model families. Added the newly registered [FunFormer study](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.09.765190): a supervised regulatory-sequence family that compresses 1-50-kb inputs to 500 tokens and trains separate multi-track classifiers on human, pig, cattle, chicken, Atlantic salmon, and zebrafish. Its [public repository](https://github.com/SciFarmers/funformer/tree/6f85d7c07b1b390972d27d16cfb3ae182ac40270) provides the full training/evaluation pipeline, chromosome-held-out splits, final per-track results, figure/table regeneration, 23 cross-species directions over 359 matched channels, flank interventions, and human/pig QTL scoring. A checksum-protected 7.7-GB working archive provides 30 selected runs and prediction caches, but remains on Google Drive pending a permanent deposit; source genomes and EN-TEx/FAANG/AQUA-FAANG/GEO tracks are not redistributed, and the repository declares no licence. Counted as one supervised model family, one training-data resource, and one evaluation suite. Post-boundary CENO commits only repair padded and long-context inference; the new `genomic-fm-lab` is an educational small-model exercise without a paper or released research checkpoint. [kron-dna](https://github.com/antonkulaga/kron-fm) now reports interim 15k/50k-step results and bootstrap intervals, but its root status still says nothing is pretrained, no weights or result artifacts are public, and the SynCodonLM run/evaluation harness remain unfinished. [MarinDNA exp593](https://github.com/Open-Athena/marin-dna/pull/594) is still a draft PR without released weights, [ALBench-S2F](https://github.com/trchristensen-99/ALBench-S2F) remains an active unlicensed experiment, and the refreshed DNA-50M/175M cards remain undocumented. Catalogue totals: **205 models, 149 corpora, and 157 benchmarks**.
- **[2026-11-10]** Reconciled the November 9 PhaGen/CreatiPhage follow-up and the earlier same-day EvSpark commit, then extended the primary-source audit through 09:00 +09:00. The [arXiv genomics listing](https://arxiv.org/list/q-bio.GN/recent) still ends on November 6, the [November 9–10 bioRxiv interval](https://api.biorxiv.org/details/biorxiv/2026-11-09/2026-11-10/0/json) grew from 206 to 252 indexed records, and authenticated GitHub searches plus current Hugging Face metadata were screened. Retained [EvSpark](https://github.com/dhnihaoya/EvSpark) as an MIT-licensed toolkit rather than a model or biological benchmark: it supplies distilled speculative-decoding drafters for frozen Evo2 targets and a 24-prompt reproduction subset of the reported 48-prompt evaluation. Added [RNAtomy](https://github.com/Azam-Shi/RNAtomy), a 75.6M-parameter RNA encoder with 12 bidirectional Mamba blocks, four shifted-window-attention blocks, and joint masked reconstruction of pairwise geometry and 23-dimensional residue descriptors. Its [CC BY 4.0 Zenodo release](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.23968854) provides a 1.2-GB checkpoint, FASTA sequences, descriptors, and example pairwise maps. The documentation reports 200,158 filtered RNAcentral sequences split 180,142/20,016, but the archive documentation instead lists 246,458 FASTAs and descriptors for 114,936 RNAs; the complete approximately 130-GB map collection is request-only, no pretraining script or public paper was located, and the repository's stated MIT link points to a missing licence file. Added the six released [RNAtomy structural/functional task snapshots](https://github.com/Andrey199123/RNA-Structural-and-Functional-Task-Datasets) as an evaluation resource while recording that mean-ribosome-load data, model training/prediction code, and reported results are absent. Recovered [DNA GBST-BERT](https://github.com/OMAR622-ai/dna-xlstm-charna), a 2.107M-parameter ChaRNABERT-style encoder pretrained from scratch for 10.5B sampled hg38 tokens at 1,024-nt context; Apache-2.0 code, final resumable checkpoints, detailed reports, 18-task Nucleotide Transformer results over ten seeds, and fine-tuned checkpoints are public, but no archival paper was found and its evaluation reuses the existing benchmark row. Added [GNE-Audit](https://github.com/biubiutomato/gne-audit), an MIT-licensed frozen 787-sequence bacterial marker-gene panel with HyenaDNA-tiny and composition-baseline embeddings, exact nearest-neighbour audit code, result tables, and figures; the repository cites a BIBM 2026 workshop paper, but no public manuscript, DOI, or tagged release was located. The new [AlphaGenome promoter-perturbation preprint](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.06.764851) evaluates 2,111 promoter–gene pairs across 20 tissues, but its [linked repository](https://github.com/SpielmannLab/S2F_PromoterDisruption) currently contains only a placeholder README promising scripts, with no data, code, licence, or release. [kron-dna](https://github.com/antonkulaga/kron-fm) added SynCodonLM streaming preparation and resumable training, but its own status still says nothing is pretrained and the evaluation harness remains planned; [MarinDNA exp593](https://github.com/Open-Athena/marin-dna/pull/594) remains a draft pull request, and [ALBench-S2F](https://github.com/trchristensen-99/ALBench-S2F) still has no declared repository licence. Recent MicroGlot and C3P commits only add figures, while the refreshed DNA-50M/175M cards remain undocumented. Catalogue totals: **204 models, 148 corpora, and 155 benchmarks**.
- **[2026-11-09]** Extended the primary-source audit through 09:08 +09:00 after reconciling the earlier same-day ModernGENA update. The latest [arXiv genomics listing](https://arxiv.org/list/q-bio.GN/recent) still ends on November 6; the [November 8–9 bioRxiv interval](https://api.biorxiv.org/details/biorxiv/2026-11-08/2026-11-09/0/json) now contains two unrelated systems-biology/immunology records; authenticated GitHub searches and current Hugging Face DNA/RNA model and dataset metadata were also screened. Recovered [PhaGen](https://github.com/ai4nucleome/PhaGen), a 152.4M-parameter, 131,072-token hierarchical masked-diffusion model trained on 98,665 phage genomes (4.80 Gbp) from MGV, GPD, and NCBI. Its Apache-2.0 code, [public checkpoint](https://huggingface.co/YusenWithR/PhaGen), training configuration, and [1.8-GB Zenodo v1 archive](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.23477083) release the evaluation inputs, processed data, results, and scripts for genome generation plus seven sequence-understanding tasks; source datasets retain their upstream terms and no public manuscript DOI or preprint was located. Added the newly released [CreatiPhage and CreatiPhage Diffusion DNA-LM (CPDD)](https://github.com/dhnihaoya/CreatiPhage-Diffusion-DNA-Lm): a 144.5M-parameter autoregressive phage model and its 147.5M-parameter bidirectional discrete-diffusion extension, both with 98,304-token context, Apache-2.0 code, CC BY 4.0 weights, and a public technical report. Their distinct megaDNA-lineage reconstruction contains 99,429 records (4.77 Gbp) with a documented 94,469/4,960 autoregressive split. The report's 522-CDS/70-cluster comparison is not counted as another benchmark because the repository explicitly omits the complete panel and only exposes diagnostic reconstruction code. Retained the earlier [ModernGENA](https://github.com/AIRI-Institute/GENA_LM) paper/checkpoint/corpus-recipe refresh. The post-boundary [kron-dna](https://github.com/antonkulaga/kron-fm) release is an end-to-end training skeleton with no pretrained model, completed corpus pipeline, or evaluation harness; MarinDNA exp593 remains a draft training branch without a released final checkpoint/evaluation; ALBench-S2F still lacks a top-level licence and paper; and the latest AMP 2026 commits only change slide labels and chart colouring without altering benchmark data or results. The newly surfaced regulatory-DNA, plant polyadenylation, and population-genetics repositories remain small-model/downstream audits rather than additional foundation-model resources, while recent Hub activity was limited to consumer-genetics LLM and single-cell count data. Catalogue totals: **202 models, 147 corpora, and 153 benchmarks**.
- **[2026-11-08]** Reconciled the November 7 PDLM/AMP follow-up and extended the primary-source audit through 08:59 +09:00. The [arXiv genomics feed](https://arxiv.org/list/q-bio.GN/recent) still ends with its November 6 listing, while the [November 7–8 bioRxiv interval](https://api.biorxiv.org/details/biorxiv/2026-11-07/2026-11-08/0/json) still contains 30 records; authenticated GitHub repository searches and current Hugging Face DNA-model/genomic-dataset metadata were also screened. Recovered [TadA-Bench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02624), an ICML 2026 benchmark built from 31 directed-evolution rounds with aligned DNA, RNA, and protein views. Its Apache-2.0 [code](https://github.com/shiyegao/TadA-Bench) and [dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/JinGao/TadA-Bench) provide fixed chronological partitions, NT-50M/Carbon-500M baselines, and ranking metrics; official data revision is `07168448caaafab4efb26eca04ec3e503edf1c04`. Each nucleotide view has 729,302/148,014/149,884 train/validation/test examples, with rounds 1–27/28/29–31 respectively. Counted as a task-specific sequence benchmark only, not as a new model or pretraining corpus. The new permanent [MarinDNA exp593 branch](https://github.com/Open-Athena/marin-dna/pull/594) provides a tested augmented-DNA training pipeline and has submitted its production run, but no trained checkpoint or final evaluation release exists yet. The [GeneCAD revision models](https://huggingface.co/plantcad/genecad_revision_models) are supervised gene-structure checkpoints initialized from PlantCAD2 rather than an independent pretrained family or standalone benchmark. [OpenOmicsBench](https://github.com/vxxqv/openomicsbench) is a software-testing/teaching collection; [ALBench-S2F](https://github.com/trchristensen-99/ALBench-S2F) has a `colab-d20k` tag but no declared repository licence and remains under review for catalogue inclusion; and the [RNA transcript-dynamics study](https://github.com/hidenori-tani/rna-foundation-grounding-benchmark) reports several surrogate representations rather than direct evaluations of the named full models. PDLM still has no public research checkpoints or complete scientific evaluation release, while RNAcompass still lacks a public manuscript, training code, and complete evaluation assets. These candidates were therefore not promoted to additional catalogue rows. Catalogue totals: **200 models, 145 corpora, and 152 benchmarks**.
- **[2026-11-07]** Extended the primary-source audit through 09:14 +09:00 after reconciling the earlier same-day update. The [arXiv genomics feed](https://arxiv.org/list/q-bio.GN/recent) now contains two November 6 entries, the paginated [November 6–7 bioRxiv interval](https://api.biorxiv.org/details/biorxiv/2026-11-06/2026-11-07/0/json) grew from 190 to 272 records, and authenticated GitHub repository search plus current Hugging Face metadata were screened. Retained the addition of [MirrorDNA](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.04.764207), its released checkpoints and strand-consistency evaluation, the enrichment of the existing hg38 corpus row, and the [MarinDNA exp586](https://huggingface.co/marin-dna/marin-dna-exp586) 216B-token checkpoint. Added the newly posted [Protein–DNA Language Models (PDLMs)](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.05.763984): four 35M/150M ESM2-initialized base/domain variants that jointly encode protein and DNA and continue masked pretraining on 51,329 filtered pairs from BioLiP2, REBASE, CIS-BP, and AptaDB, split 46,224/2,547/2,558 with identical proteins kept within one partition. The [public core package](https://github.com/xiongyf520/PDLM) provides model, training, scoring, embedding, synthetic-demo, and offline-test code, but the four research checkpoints remain private; the full training data, manuscript-specific predictors, processed evaluation sets, scientific benchmark code, and software/model licences are not public. Consequently, the documented training resource is counted, but the paper's downstream analyses are not promoted to a standalone benchmark row. Refreshed the existing [AMP 2026 rare-disease benchmark](https://github.com/rewire-bio/amp-2026-studies/tree/amp-2026-v7) to fixed tag `amp-2026-v7`, which leaves the 16 v6 main result files unchanged and adds an explicitly post hoc 0.01%–5% allele-frequency sensitivity sweep with data, results, logs, tests, and a deviation record. [IsoAlign](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00241), [MUSS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08086), raw-signal [Brieno](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.05.764536), the structural RNA–protein interface study [RPFace](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.04.764332), and the still unversioned/unlicensed ALBench-S2F remain adaptations, out-of-scope modalities, task-specific studies, or incomplete releases rather than additional catalogue entries. Catalogue totals: **200 models, 145 corpora, and 151 benchmarks**.
- **[2026-11-06]** Extended the primary-source audit through 09:05 +09:00 after reconciling the earlier same-day update. The [arXiv genomics feed](https://arxiv.org/list/q-bio.GN/recent) still ends with its four November 5 submissions, while the [November 5–6 bioRxiv interval](https://api.biorxiv.org/details/biorxiv/2026-11-05/2026-11-06/0/json) grew from 122 to 125 records; authenticated GitHub repository searches and current Hugging Face metadata were also screened. Added [HaploLM and HaploLM-VL](https://github.com/FaiasPromit/HaploLM), 20.6M- and 11.2M-parameter causal mitochondrial DNA models trained only to predict full sequences or variant lists, with MIT-licensed code and [released checkpoints](https://huggingface.co/FaiasPromit/HaploLM). Added their reproducible training resource: 49,310 quality-filtered human mitochondrial genomes from GenBank, selected through MITOMAP's full-length accession list, with exact duplicates grouped and six complete Mitotree branches excluded from training. Added the accompanying maternal-phylogeny evaluation, which releases build/scoring scripts, fixed result tables, frequency-matched and carrier-versus-relative tests, held-out-branch negative controls, and comparisons with Evo 2, NTv3, and mtDNA-FM. The sequences are fetched from GenBank and Mitotree labels must be rebuilt under their upstream terms; no formal HaploLM paper or archival release was located, so the entry is grounded in the public repository and model card and marked accordingly. Retained the earlier addition of the [RPI-Zero benchmark](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.04.764286), whose released code and CC BY 4.0 [Zenodo dataset](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.23748141) provide 2,074,753 RNA–protein examples with cluster-separated tests, and retained the structRFM refresh to its [Nature paper](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41586-026-11210-2) and current archives. [RNAcompass](https://github.com/ZhaoRB1/RNAcompass) now provides a licensed inference package and two weights but still lacks a public manuscript, training code, and complete evaluation assets; [genome-robustness](https://github.com/Cyh1368/genome-robustness) is only a project proposal; and ALBench-S2F still lacks a paper, top-level licence, and stable study release. [VenusGenome](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05405), [BIOTRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.05418), and [GeneHop](https://arxiv.org/html/2611.05401v1) remain protein-embedding, abundance-estimation, or unreleased downstream resources. Catalogue totals: **198 models, 144 corpora, and 150 benchmarks**.
- **[2026-11-05]** Extended the primary-source audit through 09:06 +09:00 across the current [arXiv genomics feed](https://arxiv.org/list/q-bio.GN/recent) through its November 4 listing, all 356 records now returned by the [November 4–5 bioRxiv interval](https://api.biorxiv.org/details/biorxiv/2026-11-04/2026-11-05/0/json), authenticated GitHub repository searches and changes, and recent Hugging Face DNA-model and genome-dataset metadata. Recovered the [DNA barcoding classification evaluation suite](https://github.com/LorisNanni/Integrating-Foundation-Models-and-Feature-Based-Neural-Networks-for-DNA-Barcoding-Classification), first published as a repository in January 2026, with released barcode datasets, explicit Fish/Beetle ten-fold partitions, seen-species and unseen-genus Insecta protocols, and BarcodeMAE fine-tuning alongside feature-based baselines. This is a task-specific evaluation resource, not an independent foundation model or pretraining corpus; the repository has no declared licence. Added the November 2-submitted [BEND 2.0 paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03806), which documents eight tasks built on five experimental datasets, to the existing benchmark row. Refreshed the previously catalogued [DNA FM Composition-Controlled Evaluation](https://github.com/vincenzocivale/genome-cgr-embedding) after its November 4 camera-ready repository update supplied reproducible analysis code, small result tables, all 285 fixed-6-mer-plus-embedding result cells, source-hash provenance manifests, and validation scripts. The release does not redistribute source datasets, checkpoints, cached embeddings, or run logs, does not independently rerun every training-stage result, and declares no repository licence. Newly posted [MicrobeRT](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.03.763945) fine-tunes Nucleotide Transformer, DNABERT, and HyenaDNA on three metagenomic classification tasks rather than establishing a new pretrained family; the newly updated [ALBench-S2F](https://github.com/trchristensen-99/ALBench-S2F) remains an active-learning experiment without a paper, top-level licence, or stable release. GPBench evaluates genotype/SNP predictors; CellReg3D integrates an existing DNA encoder with Hi-C; Evo 2 few-shot regulatory design is an adaptation/application; and Top2All uses expression profiles. These do not establish new raw-nucleotide foundation-model families, standalone pretraining resources, or additional general-purpose benchmarks. Catalogue totals: **197 models, 143 corpora, and 148 benchmarks**.
- **[2026-11-04]** Extended the primary-source audit through 10:57 +09:00 across the current [arXiv `q-bio.GN`](https://arxiv.org/list/q-bio.GN/recent) feed (latest indexed submission: November 2), all 30 records in the 2026-11-03 to 2026-11-04 [bioRxiv interval](https://api.biorxiv.org/details/biorxiv/2026-11-03/2026-11-04/0/json), authenticated GitHub repository search and changes, Hugging Face metadata, Crossref, and official project pages. Added the public [RNAChat text–RNA training corpus](https://huggingface.co/JWJPKU/RNAChat-corpus): 1,509,419 bilingual natural-language/RNA pairs across mRNA, miRNA, tRNA, snRNA, snoRNA, rRNA, and other ncRNA classes, with structured conditions, packed sequences, source documentation, and leakage-unit-aware train/validation/test splits. Its `license: other` value is a placeholder, upstream terms remain source-specific, some text is Qwen2.5-paraphrased, and the linked RNAChat-v7 model and training code are not public. The bioRxiv update supplied version 3 of [Eterna100-V1/V2](https://doi.org/10.1101/2021.08.26.457839); its MIT repository fixes ViennaRNA-engine provenance, provides the two 100-target inverse-folding suites, six-algorithm evaluation code, player-constraint metadata, and constraint-valid sample solutions. A historical reconciliation also recovered the MIT-licensed [sequence-to-function CRISPRi benchmark](https://github.com/Al-Murphy/seq2func_crispri_eval), which releases metadata, tests, prediction tables, and end-to-end zero-shot evaluation of Enformer, Borzoi/Flashzoi, NTv3, and AlphaGenome on the Fulco and Gasperini enhancer-perturbation screens, but has no standalone paper or tagged release. Refreshed the existing MarinDNA family with the Apache-2.0 [exp586 1.5B-60M checkpoint](https://huggingface.co/plantcad/marin-dna-exp586-1.5B-60M), a 1.59B-total/approximately 60.6M-active sparse-MoE model trained for 1,000,000,192,512 input tokens at 8,192-token context, plus its [216.16B-token endpoint archive](https://huggingface.co/marin-dna/marin-dna-exp586); this is a new checkpoint within the catalogued family rather than a separate model row, and its PlantCAD2 comparison remains an open pull request. [Wreath](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41588-026-02807-4), DNABERT-2 weight-space programming, unofficial OInvFold exports, `rna-twin`, and refreshed DNA-50M/175M uploads remain downstream, task-specific, expression-matrix, or insufficiently documented resources. Catalogue totals: **197 models, 143 corpora, and 147 benchmarks**.

- **[2026-11-02]** Extended the primary-source audit through 09:05 +09:00 across the current 13-entry [arXiv `q-bio.GN`](https://arxiv.org/list/q-bio.GN/recent) index (latest indexed submission: October 30), all 21 records now exposed by the 2026-11-01 to 2026-11-02 [bioRxiv interval](https://api.biorxiv.org/details/biorxiv/2026-11-01/2026-11-02/0/json), authenticated GitHub repository search and changes, Hugging Face metadata, and official project pages. - **[2026-10-19]** Extended the primary-source audit through 09:03 +09:00. Catalog totals: **178 models, 127 corpora, and 116 benchmarks**.
- **[2026-10-16]** Extended the primary-source audit through 09:02 +09:00. The `q-bio.GN` records remained downstream perturbation, descriptive, database, or security/tooling work, but a broader `cs.LG` search recovered [*What DNA Foundation Models Learn Beyond Sequence Composition*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15711), accepted to the NeurIPS 2026 Evaluations & Datasets Track. Its [public code](https://github.com/vincenzocivale/genome-cgr-embedding) adds fixed and multi-scale *k*-mer controls, matched incremental-utility tests, ridge-recoverability diagnostics, and exact 6-mer-preserving perturbations for five frozen DNA foundation models across 57 inherited classification datasets and two exploratory expression tasks. Because it reuses public benchmark datasets, it is counted as a distinct evaluation resource but not as a new corpus. Screening all 124 bioRxiv records posted on 2026-10-15 plus post-boundary GitHub and Hugging Face candidates found no additional independently documented model, standalone pretraining corpus, or benchmark. Catalog totals: **175 models, 125 corpora, and 115 benchmarks**.

- **[2026-10-14]** Extended the primary-source audit through 09:14 +09:00. - **[2026-10-10]** Extended the primary-source audit through 09:01 +09:00 across [arXiv `q-bio.GN`](https://arxiv.org/list/q-bio.GN/recent), the complete 2026-10-09 bioRxiv batch, GitHub, and Hugging Face. The latest arXiv candidates remain variant-interpretation agents, single-cell methods, or sequence-indexing tools; the new undocumented `DNABERT2-SNR` checkpoint has no model card or provenance and remains uncounted. Catalog totals: **131 models, 84 corpora, 73 benchmarks**.
- **[2026-09-25]** Re-ran the primary-source audit through 09:06 +08:00 across arXiv `q-bio.GN`, bioRxiv genomics, GitHub, and Hugging Face. [Motif-Vocab](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28386) remains a tokenizer and inductive-bias method rather than a separately released foundation model; [GLM-Missense](https://github.com/yaqisu/GLM-Missense_MetaMissense) and [Sysgen-GLM](https://github.com/giokepa/Sysgen-GLM-for-Indels) are downstream variant-effect adaptations, while [Genova](https://github.com/glbala87/Genova) is an undocumented framework without a citable model lineage. [KmerFormer](https://github.com/m2lab-ntu/KmerFormer), GenoQuant, Epiformer, and [Laya-Bio](https://huggingface.co/datasets/dnagpt/laya-bio) remain supervised, tooling-only, or closed-set resources rather than new general-purpose foundation models or benchmarks. No additional independent DNA/RNA foundation-model family, standalone pretraining corpus, or general-purpose benchmark with comparable primary documentation was identified. Catalog totals remain: **130 models, 83 corpora, and 71 benchmarks**.
- **[2026-09-24]** Added the documented [LOCALE SRA retrieval benchmark](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.05.12.724581), covering 50-, 500-, and 4,571-accession search with clean and 5%/10% mutated DNA-read queries, plus LOCALE's public SRA-contig contrastive-training resource and build scripts. LOCALE is a DNABERT-2 adaptation rather than an independent foundation-model family, so the model count is unchanged. Rechecked arXiv `q-bio.GN`, bioRxiv genomics, GitHub, and Hugging Face through 2026-09-24; Laya-Bio was screened out as a short closed-set experiment and GenoQuant as a compression toolkit rather than a general benchmark. Catalog totals: **130 models, 83 corpora, 71 benchmarks**.
- **[2026-09-23]** Added [Minerva-MLM](https://github.com/garykbrixi/minerva), a public 650M-parameter mixed DNA/protein masked language model for coevolutionary mining, with 4,096- and 8,192-token checkpoints and interaction heads for base pairing, protein contacts, and repeats. Added its documented 1.3-trillion-token (~3.4-Tb) mixed-genome training resource; no standalone benchmark was added because the release currently exposes model heads and examples rather than a citable evaluation suite. Rechecked arXiv `q-bio.GN`, bioRxiv genomics, GitHub, and Hugging Face through 2026-09-23; ModernGENA, JEPA-DNA, DNT, DNA-MFM, and the 2026-09-22 biosynthetic-assembly-line application were already catalogued or screened as downstream use. Catalog totals: **130 models, 82 corpora, 70 benchmarks**.
- **[2026-09-22]** Added [GenoME](https://doi.org/10.1093/nar/gkag902), a multimodal mixture-of-experts model that conditions 1-Mb human DNA on cell-type-specific ATAC/DNase signals and predicts RNA-seq, ChIP-seq, Hi-C, and splice-junction profiles, together with its hg38/ENCODE multimodal training resource and perturbation/evaluation suite. Rechecked arXiv `q-bio.GN` (latest indexed submission: 2026-09-17), Europe PMC, GitHub, and Hugging Face through 2026-09-22. [EvSpark](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.09.02.749017v1) was screened out as inference acceleration; [MicroGlot](https://github.com/athanzli/MicroGlot), [BarcodeMAE+](https://github.com/bioscan-ml/BarcodeMAE-plus), DNALaCT checkpoints, and DNA-50M/175M cards remain insufficiently documented, while no additional standalone benchmark with comparable provenance was found. Catalog totals: **129 models, 81 corpora, 70 benchmarks**.
- **[2026-09-21]** Rechecked arXiv, bioRxiv, GitHub, Hugging Face, and the nucleotide-model landscape through 2026-09-21. Recovered four previously missing, source-grounded families: [BarcodeBERT](https://github.com/bioscan-ml/BarcodeBERT), [BarcodeMamba](https://github.com/bioscan-ml/BarcodeMamba), [BarcodeMamba+](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.15931), and [mtDNA-FM](https://github.com/vthawfeek/mtdna-foundation-model) (public GitHub/Hugging Face release; no formal paper located). Added the Canadian invertebrate, fungal ITS, and mitochondrial pretraining resources plus their taxonomic-transfer, fungal-shift, and mtDNA evaluation suites. No additional independent post-2026-09-20 release with comparable primary documentation was identified; BarcodeMAE+ (no paper or checkpoint release) and embedding/toolkit repositories were screened out. Catalog totals: **128 models, 80 corpora, 69 benchmarks**.
- **[2026-09-21]** Audited arXiv `q-bio.GN` (latest indexed submission: 2026-09-17), bioRxiv, GitHub, and Hugging Face through 2026-09-21. Rechecked the recently surfaced ModernGENA, JEPA-DNA, DNA-MFM, Carbon, GFMBench-API, HViLM, FishCaduceus, and BatatasLM entries; all are already catalogued with their available model, corpus, and evaluation links. No additional independent DNA/RNA foundation-model family, standalone pretraining corpus, or general-purpose benchmark with comparable primary documentation was identified after the 2026-09-20 update. Catalog totals remain: **124 models, 77 corpora, 66 benchmarks**.
- **[2026-09-20]** Rechecked arXiv `q-bio.GN` (latest indexed submission: 2026-09-17) together with bioRxiv, GitHub, and Hugging Face through 2026-09-20. Added three previously missing documented families: [HViLM](https://github.com/duttaprat/HViLM), [FishCaduceus](https://huggingface.co/FishCaduceus/FishCaduceus-20L-512), and [BatatasLM](https://github.com/Weiyiliang/BatatasLM), plus their public pretraining corpora and evaluation resources ([HVUE v2](https://huggingface.co/datasets/duttaprat/HVUE-v2), FishCaduceus functional-site/evolutionary-constraint datasets, and [Batatas_GUE](https://huggingface.co/datasets/BatatasLM/Batatas_GUE)). FishCaduceus and BatatasLM currently cite manuscripts in preparation, so their entries rely on the released code, model cards, and datasets without invented publication metadata. No additional independent post-2026-09-19 release met the inclusion standard. Catalog totals: **124 models, 77 corpora, 66 benchmarks**.
- **[2026-09-19]** Audited arXiv, bioRxiv, Nature Portfolio, GitHub, and Hugging Face for releases through 2026-09-19. No additional independent DNA/RNA foundation-model family with comparable primary documentation was identified after the 2026-09-18 update; task-specific or downstream systems such as ViSTA, resLens, Genolator, ATSFormer, Gengram, and the undocumented TSS-Benchmark repository were screened out. Recovered two public GPN training resources ([animal-promoter](https://huggingface.co/datasets/songlab/gpn-animal-promoter-dataset) and [GPN-MSA-Sapiens](https://huggingface.co/datasets/songlab/gpn-msa-sapiens-dataset)) plus two previously missing evaluation resources ([GV-Rep](https://proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2024/hash/f2b86cbc0b3e31dd001aeb516afbe1e2-Abstract-Datasets_and_Benchmarks_Track.html) and [TraitGym](https://github.com/songlab-cal/TraitGym)). Catalog totals: **121 models, 74 corpora, 62 benchmarks**.
- **[2026-09-18]** Added the newly released [FungiHelixSeek](https://github.com/zhangtaolab/FungiHelixSeek), a 263M-parameter fungal DNA foundation model with 8,192-bp context, single-nucleotide MLM, hybrid linear-attention/Transformer layers, and sparse MoE routing. Added its fungal-genome pretraining resource and 12-task fungal epigenomic benchmark with released fine-tuning datasets. Also recovered the documented [C3P](https://github.com/dufaultc/contrastive-promoter-protein-pretraining) contrastive promoter–protein model family, its 97.7M-pair bacterial corpus, and its regulatory/co-regulated retrieval evaluations. A 2026-09-15–18 screen of arXiv, bioRxiv, GitHub, and Hugging Face found no other new independent DNA/RNA foundation-model family with comparable primary documentation. Catalog totals: **121 models, 72 corpora, 60 benchmarks**.
- **[2026-09-17]** Filled three documented model-family gaps: Shorkie_LM/Shorkie, EVA, and the newly posted EukaUTR; added their released pretraining corpora and evaluation resources, plus the published miRBench benchmark. Refreshed GenomeOcean with the official 15.13B-parameter sparse-MoE checkpoint released on 2026-09-16. A same-day screen of arXiv, bioRxiv, GitHub, and Hugging Face found no newer independent DNA/RNA foundation-model family with verifiable primary documentation. Catalog totals: **119 models, 70 corpora, 58 benchmarks**. AptaBench was reviewed but deferred until its non-anonymous paper/project citation is stable.
- **[2026-09-16]** Added RIBOSPAN (1.61B parameters, native context up to 10,240 nt), MIMIC (multimodal DNA/RNA/protein), Orthrus, Helix-mRNA, and DGRNA; added the RIBOSPAN-FM corpus and MIMIC's documented LORE resource; and added mRNABench, RNAGym, BEACON, and the RIBOSPAN long-context representation benchmark. Catalog totals: **116 models, 67 corpora, 54 benchmarks**. [HyCoSeq](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16925) (submitted 2026-09-15) was screened out because it explicitly uses no large-scale genomic pretraining; [GeneScope-FM](https://github.com/codewithPauline/GeneScope-FM)'s promoter pilot, the [viral-screening benchmark](https://github.com/andrewgeorge11/viral-screening-benchmark), and undocumented [DNA-50M/175M cards](https://huggingface.co/reaperdoesntknow/DNA-50M) were also excluded as non-foundation or insufficiently documented.
- **[2026-09-15]** Added Mnemos-DNA (2.3M-parameter, 131-kbp-context linear-attention model) and TomatoPGFM (479M-parameter graph-conditioned model trained on a 66-accession tomato pangenome), including TomatoPGFM's processed evaluation resources; added DNT's diploid genotype resources and compound-heterozygous benchmark; and refreshed GenoJEPA with released Tiny/Base checkpoints, its 850-genome pretraining resource, and 55-task evaluation benchmark. Catalog totals: **111 models, 65 corpora, 50 benchmarks**. The newly posted maize stress-response model was reviewed but not listed separately because it is an AgroNT adaptation rather than a new pretrained foundation model; unofficial Evo2 ports and EvSpark drafters were screened out as conversions or decoding aids rather than new pretrained models.
- **[2026-09-14]** Filled fourteen historical gaps found by rechecking primary papers and released repositories: added VQDNA, CD-GPT, ConvNova, CARMANIA, Deep-Plant, PatchDNA, DNAZEN, MergeDNA, BacHyenaDNA, DNAGPT2, TrinityDNA, NucEL, Nucleotide GPT, and EnCodon (CodonFM); added the DNABERT-2 multi-species, Scorpio Gene-Taxa, Deep-Plant, and CodonFM RefSeq CDS corpora; and added Scorpio Gene-Taxa, the TrinityDNA CDS, and BGCs-Bench evaluations. Catalog totals: **108 models, 63 corpora, 47 benchmarks**.
- **[2026-09-13]** Added the T2T-LIN/YAO-LIN benchmark and MarinDNA (including its 1.12B m5.1 model, scaling family, pretraining corpus, and variant-effect evaluations). Catalog totals: **94 models, 59 corpora, 44 benchmarks**.

## Tag Legend

### Model design tags

- ![CLM](https://img.shields.io/badge/Objective-CLM-7c3aed) causal language modeling
- ![NTP](https://img.shields.io/badge/Objective-NTP-7c3aed) causal next-token prediction
- ![MLM](https://img.shields.io/badge/Objective-MLM-2563eb) masked language modeling
- ![AR+MLM](https://img.shields.io/badge/Objective-AR%2BMLM-9333ea) hybrid autoregressive and masked objectives
- ![Diffusion](https://img.shields.io/badge/Objective-Diffusion-db2777) discrete diffusion or diffusion-style sequence modeling
- ![Contrastive](https://img.shields.io/badge/Objective-Contrastive-0891b2) species-aware or representation contrast
- ![ELECTRA RTD](https://img.shields.io/badge/Objective-ELECTRA_RTD-475569) replaced-token detection with a generator/discriminator objective
- ![Auxiliary](https://img.shields.io/badge/Objective-Auxiliary-475569) additional supervised or biological objectives
- ![Supervised](https://img.shields.io/badge/Objective-Supervised-16a34a) supervised sequence-to-function or multi-task pretraining

### Biological scope tags

- ![Human/Eukaryotic](https://img.shields.io/badge/Corpus-Human%2FEukaryotic-15803d) human or eukaryotic reference-centered data
- ![Multi-species](https://img.shields.io/badge/Corpus-Multi--species-0f766e) genomes across multiple species
- ![Prokaryotic/Phage](https://img.shields.io/badge/Corpus-Prokaryotic%2FPhage-ca8a04) bacterial, archaeal, or phage genomes
- ![Metagenomic](https://img.shields.io/badge/Corpus-Metagenomic-854d0e) metagenomic or environmental sequences
- ![Viral/Mobile](https://img.shields.io/badge/Corpus-Viral%2FMobile-b91c1c) viruses, plasmids, phages, or mobile elements
- ![Plant](https://img.shields.io/badge/Corpus-Plant--specific-65a30d) plant-focused genomes or plant regulatory sequence data
- ![Cross-domain](https://img.shields.io/badge/Corpus-Cross--domain-4338ca) bacteria, archaea, eukaryotes, viruses, organelles, and metagenomes
