## Pretraining Corpora

<details>
<summary>Show pretraining corpora</summary>

The table below lists representative genomic pretraining corpora and data resources.

| Representative sources | Data composition | Links |
| --- | --- | --- |
| KronGenome F mixed-genome training recipe | hg38, T2T-CHM13 v2.0 and naked mole-rat T2T haplotype 1 with chr8/chr9 held out in each; 17,037 organelle genomes plus 993 held out; ten additional NTv3 species. Masked-base learning plus NTv3 functional labels, 13 ENCODE DNase tracks, GENCODE splice sites, 112-epigenome conditioning and RepeatMasker inputs. | 📋 [Pinned training description and channels](https://huggingface.co/just-dna-seq/KronGenome-F-1Mb/tree/9acfb4dad8c281e8eac65e97f056c1c338463cb1)<br>💻 [Preparation/training source](https://github.com/antonkulaga/kron-fm/tree/8ae6fe4ab0956cd737c52ad27e013ea430501099)<br>⚠️ Documented source recipe, not a verified standalone processed-corpus download; exact training window inventory and complete data snapshot unverified. Non-commercial NTv3 labels and CC BY-NC 4.0 model weights should not be mistaken for a single licence covering all upstream data |
| QSSM-DNA hg38 chromosome pretraining resource | Next-base windows sampled from hg38 chromosome 22 with whole-example reverse-complement augmentation. Main runs mix 64- and 128-nt inputs at 0.25M processed input tokens; endpoint, dense-eight and all-position conditions also run at 2.5M tokens, with a separate 256-nt ablation. Chromosomes 7/8 are reserved for validation and chromosomes 9/10 for transfer holdout construction. | 💻 [Pinned configurations and data workflow](https://github.com/Gumgo91/QSSM-DNA/tree/a3be884b6891b3db8b61006dfbe65c90361e8823)<br>📋 [Experiment grid](https://github.com/Gumgo91/QSSM-DNA/blob/a3be884b6891b3db8b61006dfbe65c90361e8823/configs/experiments.json)<br>⚠️ UCSC hg38 chromosomes are downloaded by the workflow; sampled windows and pretrained checkpoints are not distributed as a standalone corpus/model archive |
| Coarse-to-Fine Genomes | GLOBDB r232-derived microbial training resource with three at-most-8,192-token representations: 22,066,393 gene-plan examples / 548,380,795 tokens from 183,523 training genomes; 8,363,569 protein examples / 37,940,321,141 tokens from one representative genome per training genus; and 3,409,992 coding-DNA-plus-intergenic-infilling examples / 21,752,467,654 tokens from one representative per family. A seeded 1% of eligible genera is excluded into validation; public Parquet shards include taxonomy, coordinates, gene IDs, strand and per-genome provenance. | 🤗 [Revision-pinned CC BY-SA 4.0 release](https://huggingface.co/datasets/Iddah/coarse-to-fine-genomes/tree/578903a2e95d1f66f493a18d5a008c0c2c67f579)<br>📄 [GLOBDB source paper](https://doi.org/10.1093/bioadv/vbaf280)<br>⚠️ Standalone training resource without a linked model or independent paper; validation is genus-disjoint, not explicitly gene-family-disjoint |
| Apicomplexan GPN assembly resource | Paper-reported masked-nucleotide pretraining collection of 455 genome assemblies spanning 76 apicomplexan species. Exact accessions, processed sequences and split manifests not verified as public. | 📄 [Preprint](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.12.766050) |
| B-RANKO ncRNA pretraining recipe | Versioned source recipe for RNAcentral 26.0, Rfam 15.1 and GARNET; config specifies 16–2,048-nt filtering, 90% identity / 80% coverage clustering and 100,000 validation examples. These are recipe settings, not independently verified processed-corpus statistics. YAML/script key mismatch requires repair; no frozen processed bundle verified. | 💻 [Pinned source configuration](https://github.com/filipvucenik/B-RANKO/blob/5e31cb648ea186ea46daf612a7f5154e9d57c04c/branko/data/pretraining_dataset_conf.yaml)<br>💻 [Preparation script](https://github.com/filipvucenik/B-RANKO/blob/5e31cb648ea186ea46daf612a7f5154e9d57c04c/branko/data/data_preparation.sh)<br>📄 [Preprint](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.12.766109) |
| AlphaGenome 2 bulk/single-cell training resource | Human and mouse bulk assays plus over 107 million cell profiles across 68 tissues. Documented supervised sequence-to-function training collection; exact processed training bundle not independently verified as downloadable. | 🌐 [Official training description](https://deepmind.google/blog/alphagenome-2-deciphering-how-cells-read-dna-to-better-understand-disease/) |
| seqLens prokaryote-heavy corpus | 19,551 NCBI representative genomes; approximately 115B nucleotides. Public 3,200-bp chunks with 100-bp overlap; card reports 27,831,882 training, 3,478,985 validation and 3,478,986 test examples. Assembly-held-out independence is not established by the card. | 🤗 [Pinned data shards and card](https://huggingface.co/datasets/omicseye/prok_heavy/tree/9056b41efdbbdc1f1f68c2c794232ab55a913a1c)<br>📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.1093/molbev/msag139) |
| seqLens balanced multi-species corpus | Approximately 180B nucleotides; paper reports 1,354 genomes (1,166 prokaryotic and 188 eukaryotic), but repository reports 1,355. Extends the NT multi-species source with 499 archaeal and five eukaryotic genomes; Hub release contains a loader and URL manifest rather than bundled sequence shards. | 🤗 [Pinned loader and manifest](https://huggingface.co/datasets/omicseye/multi_sp/tree/65792d9ccbbe2ba92fbf41097665bbe0c50b04b4)<br>📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.1093/molbev/msag139) |
| BryoCAD bryophyte continued-pretraining resource | 17 phylogenetically diverse bryophyte genomes representing 17 orders across mosses, liverworts, and hornworts; used to continue masked pretraining of PlantCAD2 Small/Large at single-nucleotide resolution and 8,192-bp context. Documented training resource with a public 17-genome selection manifest and reconstruction scripts; users supply FASTA/GFF3 inputs, and no standalone processed corpus is distributed. | 📄 [Preprint](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.09.765485)<br>🤗 [Official corpus description](https://huggingface.co/bryocad/BryoCAD-Small/blob/4c764b3c2e0f6c08c93411928fd0a23fb9a8fdad/README.md)<br>💻 [Genome manifest and reconstruction workflow](https://github.com/yenmr/BryoCAD/tree/717711dfd81a99f697c1dda2efc1f0f77e6ac62a/pretraining) |
| ModBERT 1KGP long-read personal-genome resource | Population allele frequencies and CpG methylation levels from 242 individuals in the 1000 Genomes Long-Read Sequencing Consortium, encoded as probabilistic A/C/G/T/5mC targets for soft-target masked pretraining. The source cohort is public, but the exact 242-person selection, processed probability tensors, and training manifest are not released. | 📄 [Preprint and training description](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.12.766258)<br>🌐 [International Genome Sample Resource data portal](https://www.internationalgenome.org/data-portal/data-collections) |
| GRCh38/hg38 | Human reference assembly used as the dominant coordinate system for reference-sequence pretraining and many human regulatory benchmarks. MirrorDNA reuses Enformer training intervals with 1,024-bp windows and 10.49B sampled nucleotide tokens. MolCrawl's released genome-model cards also identify GRCh38 as their source, but do not publish the exact processed snapshot; neither use creates a new genome collection. | 🌐 [NCBI Datasets](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/datasets/genome/GCF_000001405.40/)<br>🌐 [UCSC hg38](https://genome.ucsc.edu/cgi-bin/hgGateway?db=hg38)<br>💻 [MirrorDNA sampling code and training recipe](https://github.com/NIRJHOR-DATTA/MirrorDNA)<br>💻 [MolCrawl genome preparation pipeline](https://github.com/mmai-framework-lab/MolCrawl/tree/c5f32119817301b22ec07914141225c945deec3a/molcrawl/data/genome_sequence) |
| Double-Take GRCh38 masked-LM corpus | GRCh38 single-nucleotide windows used for Double-Take's 87M-parameter bidirectional masked-language pretraining; 32-kb contexts, with chromosomes 8 and 9 held out for downstream evaluation. The paper documents the recipe but does not release a separate corpus package. | 📄 [Paper and training details](https://arxiv.org/html/2610.13073v1) |
| USM-AA alignment-aware genomic mixture | Approximately 3.2 Gbp of hg38, 32.5 Gbp from the 135-species DNABERT-2 corpus, and 31.7 Gbp of SNV/indel-derived alternative-allele content from 3,202 high-coverage 1000 Genomes samples, aligned to hg38 with Minimap2 for coordinate-, strand-, and CIGAR-aware pretraining. The paper documents the recipe, but its advertised source repository is not currently public. | 📄 [Paper and methods](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.10.10.757278) |
| GeneZip region-aware GENCODE corpus | 100 billion base pairs sampled from the human reference genome with static GENCODE promoter, CDS, UTR, exon, intron, and intergenic annotations used to supervise regional compression budgets; the public release provides the preparation/training pipeline and model checkpoints rather than a separate sequence archive. | 📄 [Paper and training details](https://arxiv.org/html/2602.17739v3)<br>💻 [Code and checkpoints](https://github.com/AndyJZhao/GeneZip) |
| MethylQUEEN ENCODE/Roadmap WGBS corpus | More than 500 million assembled methylation-encoded bisulfite-sequencing reads from over 500 human samples spanning more than 30 tissues in ENCODE and the NIH Roadmap Epigenomics project. | 📄 [Paper and methods](https://doi.org/10.1101/2024.12.26.630389)<br>🌐 [ENCODE](https://www.encodeproject.org/)<br>🌐 [Roadmap Epigenomics](https://egg2.wustl.edu/roadmap/web_portal/) |
| EpiGePT context-aware ENCODE resource | Human hg19 and hg38 sequence paired with expression profiles for 711 transcription factors and eight ENCODE epigenomic signals. The paper reports 13.3 million 128-bp bins across 28 hg19 cell types and 15.87 million bins across 104 hg38 contexts; the official portal releases pretrained models, motif scores, targets, TF expression, metadata, and tutorial assets. | 📄 [Paper and methods](https://doi.org/10.1186/s13059-024-03449-7)<br>💻 [Preparation and training code](https://github.com/ZjGaothu/EpiGePT)<br>📦 [Models and processed data](https://health.tsinghua.edu.cn/epigept/download.php) |
| MoDNA human-genome motif corpus | 4,963,161 human-reference sequences annotated with 769 HOCOMOCO v11 transcription-factor motifs of 7–25 nt for joint replaced-token detection and motif-prediction pretraining; the paper documents the resource but does not provide a standalone corpus release. | 📄 [Paper and methods](https://doi.org/10.3390/biomedinformatics4020085)<br>🌐 [HOCOMOCO v11](https://hocomoco11.autosome.org/) |
| T2T-CHM13 | Gapless telomere-to-telomere human assembly resolving centromeres, acrocentric short arms, segmental duplications, and other previously missing regions. | 🌐 [NCBI Datasets](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/datasets/genome/GCF_009914755.1/) |
| hg19 | Legacy GRCh37/UCSC human reference widely used by older epigenomic annotations, regulatory datasets, and benchmark splits. | 🌐 [UCSC hg19](https://genome.ucsc.edu/cgi-bin/hgGateway?db=hg19) |
| mm10/mm39 | Mouse reference assemblies used for mammalian regulatory sequence modeling, mouse functional genomics tracks, and cross-species transfer. | 🌐 [UCSC mm10](https://genome.ucsc.edu/cgi-bin/hgGateway?db=mm10)<br>🌐 [UCSC mm39](https://genome.ucsc.edu/cgi-bin/hgGateway?db=mm39) |
| TAIR10 | Arabidopsis thaliana reference genome and gene annotation baseline for plant sequence-function datasets. | 🌐 [TAIR10 release](https://www.arabidopsis.org/download/index-auto.jsp?dir=%2Fdownload_files%2FGenes%2FTAIR10_genome_release) |
| 1000 Genomes Project | Phased human variants and haplotypes across global populations, enabling allele-specific or variant-aware sequence construction. | 🌐 [Project portal](https://www.internationalgenome.org/) |
| hEvo population-haplotype corpus | The full hEvo model uses 4.784 billion distinct haplotype sequences reconstructed from whole-genome data for 63,809 individuals selected from 550,237 samples across eight cohorts. The public `-o` variants instead use globally deduplicated 1,024-nt phased windows from the 1000 Genomes Project and HGDP for span-masked pretraining, followed by allele-frequency-stratified SNV calibration. Processed corpora are not released separately, and pretraining/cohort-preprocessing code requires manual reviewer access. | 📄 [Preprint](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.10.19.760276)<br>🔒 [Pretraining code (reviewer-gated)](https://huggingface.co/MulinLab/hEvo-pretraining-code)<br>🌐 [1000 Genomes Project](https://www.internationalgenome.org/)<br>🌐 [HGDP](https://www.internationalgenome.org/data-portal/data-collection/hgdp)<br>🤗 [Public-data models (reviewer-gated)](https://huggingface.co/MulinLab) |
| 3,202 high-coverage human genomes | High-depth 1000 Genomes GRCh38 call set with SNVs, indels, and structural variants for population-scale sequence variation. | 📦 [30x GRCh38 call set](https://www.internationalgenome.org/data-portal/data-collection/30x-grch38) |
| UK Biobank variant-derived genomes | Participant-level genotypes used to construct variant-aware personal genomic sequences for UKBioBERT; access is controlled rather than openly downloadable. | 📄 [UKBioBERT paper](https://doi.org/10.1038/s44387-026-00103-4)<br>🌐 [UK Biobank](https://www.ukbiobank.ac.uk/enable-your-research/apply-for-access) |
| Human Pangenome Reference Consortium | Haplotype-resolved human pangenome assemblies and graph references capturing alternatives absent from a single linear reference. | 🌐 [Project portal](https://humanpangenome.org/) |
| Zoonomia 240-species alignment | Cactus whole-genome alignment and phylogeny across 240 placental mammals, supporting phylogeny-aware models such as PhyloGPN and GPN-Star. | 🌐 [Data portal](https://zoonomiaproject.org/the-data/) |
| Ensembl Genomes | Comparative genome and annotation resource for non-vertebrate eukaryotes, bacteria, plants, fungi, protists, and metazoa. | 🌐 [Portal](https://ensemblgenomes.org/) |
| NCBI RefSeq | Curated reference sequences and genome assemblies with standardised annotation across taxa. | 🌐 [RefSeq](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/refseq/) |
| NCBI GenBank | Primary archival nucleotide sequence database containing submitted genomic sequences and assemblies before RefSeq curation. | 🌐 [GenBank](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/genbank/) |
| Phytozome | JGI plant comparative genomics portal with plant genome assemblies, gene models, families, and functional annotations. | 🌐 [Portal](https://phytozome-next.jgi.doe.gov/) |
| OpenGenome | Evo pretraining corpus of prokaryotic and phage whole-genome sequences at single-nucleotide resolution. | 🤗 [Dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/LongSafari/open-genome) |
| BV-BRC/PATRIC prokaryotic genes | Approximately 110 million prokaryotic gene sequences used for GenSLMs pretraining before viral adaptation. | 🌐 [BV-BRC](https://www.bv-brc.org/)<br>📄 [GenSLMs paper](https://journals.sagepub.com/doi/10.1177/10943420231201154) |
| NCBI RefSeq Bacteria/Archaea | Curated bacterial and archaeal assemblies and annotations used for microbial genome representation. | 🌐 [RefSeq prokaryotes](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/refseq/about/prokaryotes/) |
| GenBank Prokaryotic Genomes | Broad submitted prokaryotic genome assemblies, including draft genomes and isolates with variable curation status. | 🌐 [NCBI Genome browser](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/genome/browse#!/prokaryotes/) |
| IMG/VR | Viral genome catalogue from isolate and metagenomic data, useful for phage-inclusive microbial pretraining. | 🌐 [IMG/VR](https://img.jgi.doe.gov/vr/) |
| GenomeOcean corpus | Large-scale metagenomic assemblies used by GenomeOcean, emphasising microbial contigs from environmental and host-associated samples. | 💻 [Repository](https://github.com/jgi-genomeocean/genomeocean)<br>📄 [Paper](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.1101/2025.01.30.635558v2) |
| IMG/M | Integrated microbial genomes and microbiomes resource with metagenome projects, MAGs, isolate genomes, and functional annotations. | 🌐 [IMG/M](https://img.jgi.doe.gov/m/) |
| MGnify | EMBL-EBI metagenomics resource with assembled contigs, annotations, taxonomic profiles, and biome metadata. | 🌐 [MGnify](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/metagenomics/) |
| Open MetaGenome (OMG) | Approximately 3.1 trillion base pairs assembled from IMG and MGnify, released for genomic-context language modeling with gLM2. | 🤗 [Dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/tattabio/OMG)<br>📄 [Paper](https://openreview.net/forum?id=jlzNb1iWs3) |
| JGI Metagenome | JGI project portal for environmental sequencing projects and metagenomic datasets feeding IMG/M-style resources. | 🌐 [JGI portal](https://genome.jgi.doe.gov/portal/) |
| GOLD | Genome Online Database metadata catalogue for genome and metagenome projects, sampling environments, sequencing status, and study metadata. | 🌐 [GOLD](https://gold.jgi.doe.gov/) |
| Tara Oceans | Marine microbial, viral, and plankton omics samples from global ocean expeditions, available through ENA project records. | 📦 [ENA PRJEB402](https://www.ebi.ac.uk/ena/browser/view/PRJEB402) |
| NCBI Virus | Curated virus sequence portal with genome records, isolate metadata, host information, and sequence downloads. | 🌐 [NCBI Virus](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/labs/virus/vssi/#/) |
| GISAID SARS-CoV-2 genomes | Controlled-access viral genome collection used for GenSLMs adaptation and lineage-focused modeling; reuse is governed by GISAID terms. | 🌐 [GISAID](https://gisaid.org/)<br>📄 [GenSLMs paper](https://journals.sagepub.com/doi/10.1177/10943420231201154) |
| GenBank Viral | Submitted viral nucleotide records in GenBank, covering complete genomes, segments, isolates, and partial viral sequences. | 🌐 [GenBank](https://www.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/genbank/) |
| OpenVirus | Non-redundant viral nucleotide and protein collection released with LucaVirus, comprising approximately 15.7 million sequence pairs. | 🤗 [Dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/LucaGroup/LucaVirus-OpenVirus-Gene-Prot) |
| PhagesDB | Community phage database with bacteriophage genomes, host annotations, clusters, and related metadata. | 🌐 [PhagesDB](https://phagesdb.org/) |
| PLSDB | Curated plasmid database containing plasmid sequences, host information, and metadata for mobile genetic element studies. | 🌐 [PLSDB](https://ccb-microbe.cs.uni-saarland.de/plsdb/) |
| OpenGenome2 | Cross-domain Evo 2 pretraining corpus spanning bacteria, archaea, eukaryotes, viruses, organelles, and metagenomic sequence. | 🤗 [Dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/arcinstitute/opengenome2) |
| DeepD cross-domain whole-genome corpus | 5.69 Tb of unselected whole-genome sequence spanning the domains of life, retaining genic and intergenic context for phylogeny-informed sparse-MoE pretraining at single-nucleotide resolution. The source mixture and processed corpus are not publicly released. | 📄 [Preprint](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.10.13.758170)<br>💻 [Official repository and API examples](https://github.com/biomap-research/DeepD) |
| Evo-PIN prokaryotic intergenic fine-tuning resource | Bacterial and archaeal intergenic sequences used to adapt Evo 2 for non-coding-element representations. The associated atlas pipeline maps 376 million regions from 1.3 Tbp of prokaryotic genomes and metagenomes into 5.14 million clusters; the paper and portal describe the resource, but no standalone training-corpus download is linked. | 📄 [Preprint](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.10.21.760802)<br>🌐 [COMPASS resource description](https://compass.arcinstitute.org/about)<br>🌐 [Atlas and release access](https://compass.arcinstitute.org/agents) |
| GPN animal-promoter corpus | 9.04 million fixed 512-bp promoter windows from animal reference genomes used for the GPN animal-promoter masked-language-model pretraining run. | 🤗 [Dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/songlab/gpn-animal-promoter-dataset)<br>💻 [GPN code](https://github.com/songlab-cal/gpn) |
| GPN-MSA-Sapiens training windows | 4,512,284 multi-species alignment windows derived from the multiz100way vertebrate alignment and conservation-filtered for GPN-MSA-Sapiens training. | 🤗 [Dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/songlab/gpn-msa-sapiens-dataset)<br>📄 [Paper](https://www.nature.com/articles/s41587-024-02511-w)<br>💻 [GPN code](https://github.com/songlab-cal/gpn) |
| ModernGENA vertebrate corpus | 443 RefSeq vertebrate assemblies; 353,574,093,776 bp reported. Training enriches TSS regions using windows from 16 kb upstream to 8 kb downstream, merges overlaps, includes reverse complements, and excludes ambiguous non-ACGT sequence. Documented training recipe, not a separately verified downloadable corpus package. | 📄 [Preprint](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.04.21.719816v1)<br>💻 [Official corpus recipe](https://github.com/AIRI-Institute/GENA_LM#pretraining-corpus)<br>🤗 [Model card](https://huggingface.co/AIRI-Institute/moderngena-large) |
| Genomics-FM multi-species/1000G corpus | 1.9 billion unlabeled sequences drawn from 1,160 species plus human 1000 Genomes variation for masked pretraining with an ensemble of BPE and *k*-mer genomic vocabularies. The paper documents the mixture, while the processed corpus is not released as a standalone package. | 📄 [Preprint and methods](https://doi.org/10.1101/2024.07.16.603653)<br>💻 [Pretraining code and tokenizers](https://github.com/terry-r123/Genomics-FM) |
| GenoJEPA pretraining resource | Public metadata for 850 genomes plus processed DNA sequence statistics/resources used to construct global and local views for JEPA-style genomic representation learning. | 📄 [Paper](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.04.02.716255v1)<br>💻 [Code](https://github.com/ForestsKing/GenoJEPA)<br>🤗 [Dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/ChengsenWang/GenoJEPA-Pretraining) |
| TomatoPGFM 66-accession pangenome | 54.65 Gb of assembled sequence from 66 tomato accessions, represented with pangenome-node attributes and local adjacency; LA1974 and MicroTom were held out for downstream evaluation and source assemblies are accession-linked rather than redistributed. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.08.31.748176)<br>💻 [Code](https://github.com/tomatoai2026/TomatoPGFM)<br>📦 [Processed evaluation resources](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.22033079) |
| Carbon pretraining corpus | Open DNA and RNA corpus with about 173 million sequences and 1.1 trillion nucleotides across eukaryotic, prokaryotic, mRNA, and splice-aware subsets. | 🤗 [Dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/HuggingFaceBio/carbon-pretraining-corpus) |
| FineDNA Carbon-X training resource | Public 955-GB, 372,972,233-row release covering all three Carbon-X stages: 23 source-level mid-training configs with 514.30B available tokens (302B sampled for training), an exact 203,923-conversation SFT mixture (230.3M tokens), and 98,433 verifiable RL rows (61.1M prompt tokens). It preserves the trained formatting and mixture recipes for genomic/transcript sequences, DNA-bearing biology text, English, instruction data, and DNA tasks. | 🤗 [Apache-2.0 dataset and detailed card](https://huggingface.co/datasets/HuggingFaceBio/FineDNA)<br>💻 [Training recipes](https://github.com/huggingface/carbon/tree/main/carbon-x)<br>⚠️ Upstream components retain source-specific licences and terms |
| LLaMA-Gene mixed continued-pretraining resource | Documented 48-GB mixture comprising 16 GB of multi-organism DNA, 16 GB of Swiss-Prot/TrEMBL protein sequences, and 16 GB of S2ORC biology text; the public training repository also includes DNA/protein instruction data but declares no dataset card or licence. | 📄 [Paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.00471)<br>💻 [MIT-licensed preparation/training code](https://github.com/maris205/llama-gene)<br>🤗 [Released training files](https://huggingface.co/datasets/dnagpt/llama-gene-train-data) |
| OmniGene-4 mixed CPT corpus | Approximately 96 GB of released DNA, UniRef/LucaOne protein, OpenWebText replay, 3Di, and DSSP assets. The final model card documents a sampled 32.5-GB CPT run containing 8.0 GB DNA, 15.5 GB protein, 8.0 GB text, 0.4 GB structure, and 0.6 GB instruction replay. | 📄 [Preprint](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.05.12.724542)<br>🤗 [CC BY 4.0 corpus](https://huggingface.co/datasets/dnagpt/omnigene4-cpt-corpus)<br>🤗 [CPT model card](https://huggingface.co/dnagpt/OmniGene-4-CPT-v2-merged) |
| Pleiades methylated-DNA corpus | Approximately 1.9 trillion methylated and unmethylated human DNA tokens assembled from whole-genome methylation and cfDNA resources for 90M/600M/7B epigenetic foundation models. | 📄 [Paper](https://openreview.net/pdf/6a092a5ab78bf7a5d398cdbee2a8fc73146c9866.pdf)<br>🌐 [Project](https://www.primamente.com/Pleiades-July-2025) |
| EvoLen tokenized hg38 corpus | Approximately 1.09 million non-overlapping 512-token windows from hg38, released with evolution-stratified, length-aware tokenization derived from phyloP conservation regions. | 📄 [Paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08698)<br>🤗 [Dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/EvoLenTokenizer/pretrain)<br>💻 [Preparation code](https://github.com/HN020719/EvoLen/tree/main/pretrain) |
| 149-genome rice pangenome | Oryza sativa pangenome used to train OryzaG3 for rice-specific sequence generation and representation learning. | 📄 [OryzaG3 paper](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.05.22.727045v1)<br>📄 [Pangenome resource](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC12176639/) |
| BaseData metagenomes | Large metagenomic collection described as EDEN's training source; the underlying sequence corpus and access conditions are not publicly specified. | 📄 [EDEN paper](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.01.12.699009v1) |
| ENCODE 6,389 functional genomics tracks | Human hg38-aligned DNase-seq, ATAC-seq, TF ChIP-seq, and histone-modification tracks used for supervised multi-task pretraining of SUCCEED. | 📄 [SUCCEED paper](https://www.nature.com/articles/s41467-026-73129-6)<br>💻 [Target metadata](https://github.com/bioczsun/SUCCEED/blob/main/data/target_6389.txt) |
| GenoME human multimodal regulatory corpus | Human GRCh38 sequence paired with 89 cell-type ATAC/DNase groups and matched ENCODE RNA-seq, ChIP-seq, Hi-C, and splice-junction tracks; 1-Mb windows use chromosome-held-out splits for multimodal supervised training and evaluation. | 📄 [NAR paper](https://doi.org/10.1093/nar/gkag902)<br>💻 [Code](https://github.com/JWei2015/GenoME)<br>🌐 [ENCODE](https://www.encodeproject.org/) |
| Cerberus human/mouse regulatory-track corpus | hg38/mm10 sequences paired with 8,361 human and 3,102 mouse RNA-seq, 3′ RNA-seq, CAGE, ChIP-seq, DNase-seq, CLIP-seq, and ATAC-seq coverage tracks from bulk and single-cell-pseudobulk sources; the complete source/accession inventory is public, while processed training arrays remain pending. | 📄 [Paper and methods](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.10.02.756377)<br>💻 [Track inventories and training recipe](https://github.com/calico/baskerville/tree/main/releases/cerberus) |
| Bolero atlas-conditioned regulatory resource | Reference-genome sequence paired with atlas-derived cell-state embeddings and pseudobulk chromatin-accessibility and transcript-abundance targets from 10.7 million cells in 33 datasets across six mammals; the public repository also provides reference tracks, QTL/variant resources, and differential-analysis artifacts. | 📄 [Preprint](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.10.19.760026)<br>🤗 [Data assets](https://huggingface.co/datasets/arcinstitute/bolero-data)<br>🤗 [Released model configurations](https://huggingface.co/arcinstitute/bolero-models)<br>⚠️ Hub README and licence metadata are not yet provided |
| FunFormer six-vertebrate regulatory-track resource | GRCh38, Sscrofa11.1, ARS-UCD1.2, GRCg6a, Ssal_v3.1, and GRCz10 intervals paired with 423 / 80 / 95 / 97 / 301 / 37 chromatin-accessibility and histone-mark tracks from EN-TEx, FAANG, AQUA-FAANG, and GEO GSE134055. Splits hold out complete autosomes and sequences are fetched from genome FASTA at run time; the public sources and builders are documented, but the training Arrow datasets are not redistributed. | 📄 [Preprint](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.09.765190)<br>💻 [Source inventory, builders, and split protocol](https://github.com/SciFarmers/funformer/tree/6f85d7c07b1b390972d27d16cfb3ae182ac40270)<br>⚠️ Source-specific terms apply and the repository declares no licence |
| Carbon-A RefSeq eukaryotic training resource | 2,055 RefSeq assemblies from 2,047 species across mammals, other vertebrates, invertebrates, plants, fungi, and protozoa, used to train the 1.2B Carbon-A encoder; the full source corpus is described but not redistributed as a standalone dataset. | 📄 [Preprint](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.10.20.760448)<br>🤗 [Carbon-A model card](https://huggingface.co/HuggingFaceBio/Carbon-A-1.2B) |
| 422-genome rice pangenome | QC-filtered cultivated and wild Oryza assemblies used to pretrain OneGenome-Rice (OGR). | 📄 [OGR paper](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.64898/2026.04.21.719822v2)<br>💻 [Project](https://github.com/BGI-Plant/OneGenome-Rice) |
| Genos-m microbial corpus | Approximately 1.2 trillion single-nucleotide tokens from GTDB R220 representatives, public human-associated microbial genomes, high-quality human gut MAGs, and UHGV phages; about 32% of the training mixture is in-house and not publicly released. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.05.21.726868)<br>💻 [Corpus composition](https://github.com/BGI-HangzhouAI/Genos-m) |
| Minerva mixed-genome corpus | About 1.3 trillion mixed DNA/protein tokens (~3.4 Tbases) used to continue pretraining a 650M-parameter gLM2-derived model at 4,096- and 8,192-token contexts; the project documents sequence construction but does not release a separate corpus package. | 💻 [Project documentation](https://github.com/garykbrixi/minerva)<br>🤗 [Model card](https://huggingface.co/gbrixi/minerva-mlm) |
| LOCALE SRA contig training resource | Public SRA accession manifests and reproducible contig-collection scripts for contrastive local-alignment training; overlapping crops and substitution/insertion/deletion augmentations are used to adapt DNABERT-2, while the assembled training sequences are not distributed as a standalone corpus. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.05.12.724581)<br>💻 [Training-data pipeline](https://github.com/ryansynk/locale-data)<br>💻 [Model and training code](https://github.com/ryansynk/locale) |
| BacCorpus DNA and protein subsets | Open bacterial coding-region protein sequences and intergenic DNA used to train the masked and causal BacLM checkpoints. | 🤗 [Intergenic DNA](https://huggingface.co/datasets/AllTheBacteria/BacCorpus-intergenic-dna-90)<br>🤗 [Protein sequences](https://huggingface.co/datasets/AllTheBacteria/BacCorpus-prot-90)<br>🤗 [Model card](https://huggingface.co/macwiatrak/baclm-350m-masked) |
| C3P promoter–protein corpus | 97.7 million promoter–protein pairs extracted from 40,000 RefSeq bacterial genome assemblies (87.7M training pairs plus a 10.0M taxonomy-held-out validation set) for contrastive promoter representation learning. | 📄 [Paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25242)<br>💻 [Data pipeline](https://github.com/dufaultc/contrastive-promoter-protein-pretraining)<br>🤗 [100M checkpoint](https://huggingface.co/dufaultc/contrastive-promoter-protein-pretraining) |
| PDLM protein–DNA pair corpus | 51,329 filtered and deduplicated protein–DNA pairs from BioLiP2, REBASE, CIS-BP, and AptaDB, restricted to 50–1,000-aa proteins and 3–50-nt DNA sequences. The paper reports 46,224 training, 2,547 validation, and 2,558 test pairs with identical proteins confined to one partition; the public package does not include the full data or database-build pipeline. | 📄 [Preprint and methods](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.05.763984)<br>💻 [Public core package and synthetic data format](https://github.com/xiongyf520/PDLM)<br>⚠️ Source-database terms apply; no processed corpus or licence is released |
| DNA Benchmarks collection | Model-agnostic collection combining genome-scale human and rice tiling datasets, long-range/eQTL data, Genomic Benchmarks, and Nucleotide Transformer downstream tasks in reproducible official and standardized raw layouts. | 🤗 [Dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/hxxiang/dna_benchmarks)<br>📄 [Associated paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.02014) |
| OpticalDNA HG38/Rice pretraining inputs | Human HG38 and rice NIP-T2T genome documents rendered into visual pages for OpticalDNA pretraining and released checkpoint reproduction. | 💻 [Data preparation](https://github.com/HongxinXiang/OpticalDNA#-5-data-preparation)<br>🤗 [DNA Benchmarks](https://huggingface.co/datasets/hxxiang/dna_benchmarks) |
| GenART multispecies corpus | Approximately 53 billion nucleotides from 15,844 species used for masked pretraining with learned variable-length genomic token boundaries; the complete source corpus is described by the authors but is not packaged as a standalone public dataset. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.1016/j.crmeth.2026.101516)<br>💻 [Code and checkpoints](https://github.com/XMUDM/GenART) |
| Plant reference genomes and fine-tuning datasets | Plant reference genomes used by the PDLLM model family, plus nine released multi-species plant genomic datasets for downstream fine-tuning. | 💻 [Dataset index](https://github.com/zhangtaolab/Plant_DNA_LLMs/blob/main/docs/en/resources/pretrain_models.md)<br>🤗 [Hugging Face datasets](https://huggingface.co/zhangtaolab/datasets)<br>🧩 [ModelScope datasets](https://www.modelscope.cn/search?page=1&search=zhangtaolab&type=dataset) |
| ARSENAL regulatory corpus | ENCODE candidate cis-regulatory elements used for targeted short-context masked pretraining with motif-discovery regularization. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.02.05.703637)<br>💻 [Code](https://github.com/kundajelab/regulatory_lm)<br>📦 [Models/Data](https://sageb.io/ydjhqM) |
| Cypri-24 | Approximately 28.8 Gb of genome assemblies from 24 representative Cypriniformes species, used to pretrain FishMamba-1 on 15 billion tokens. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.03.09.710409)<br>💻 [Preparation code](https://github.com/lu1000001/FishMamba) |
| GenNA nucleotide-text corpus | Approximately 416 billion characters from gene-associated genomic DNA and RNA across 2,221 RefSeq eukaryotic species, paired with functional text, species metadata, and structured annotations. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.04.22.720063)<br>💻 [Preparation code](https://github.com/DrBlackZJU/GenNA) |
| PlantGFM 12-species corpus | Complete reference genomes from 12 plant species totaling approximately 10.84 billion nucleotides, used for staged autoregressive pretraining from 1 kb to 64 kb contexts. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.1002/advs.75772)<br>🤗 [Model card](https://huggingface.co/hu-lab/PlantGFM) |
| PlasmidLM Addgene corpus | Approximately 108,000 plasmid sequences from Addgene, paired with biological component specifications for prompt-conditioned autoregressive modeling and post-training. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.05.19.725242)<br>🤗 [Model card](https://huggingface.co/McClain/PlasmidLM) |
| PlasmidGPT Addgene corpus | 153,208 engineered plasmid sequences from Addgene, used for decoder-only autoregressive pretraining, embedding learning, plasmid annotation, and controlled generation. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.1126/sciadv.aee6916)<br>💻 [Code](https://github.com/lingxusb/PlasmidGPT)<br>📦 [Weights/Data](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19020226) |
| Porcine MutBERT Pig-Ref/Pig-Var | Sscrofa11.1 reference sequence plus population-aware variants from 40 wild and domestic pigs; each representation covers approximately 2.7 billion bp. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.1093/bib/bbag319)<br>💻 [Code/Data](https://github.com/ai4nucleome/pigmutbert) |
| BOTANIC-0 plant-genome corpus | Nuclear-genome assemblies from 43 phylogenetically diverse plant species used for 6-mer masked pretraining of the 100M-to-1B-parameter BOTANIC-0 family; the corpus is described but not released as a standalone package. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.02.23.706817)<br>🤗 [Model collection](https://huggingface.co/collections/living-models/botanic0) |
| PlantBiMoE 42-species corpus | Approximately 25.40 billion bp from 42 NCBI plant reference genomes, segmented into 32,768-bp windows with overlap, sequence-quality filtering, and reverse-complement augmentation; the processed corpus is described but not released as a standalone package. | 📄 [Paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.07113)<br>💻 [Code](https://github.com/HUST-Keep-Lin/PlantBiMoE)<br>🤗 [Model card](https://huggingface.co/plant-llms/PlantBiMoE) |
| Vir2vec pan-viral corpus | Curated continual-pretraining corpus of 565,747 complete genomes from 295 viral species, assembled from NCBI Virus, BV-BRC, GISAID, LANL-HIVdb, and HBVdb; the current repository documents a species-proportional 70/30 split and includes training/validation/test archives, whose completeness was not independently audited; source-specific access terms still apply. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.64898/2025.12.12.693901)<br>💻 [Pinned corpus documentation](https://github.com/pabloarozarena/Vir2vec/blob/aa027eb10ea90c4afc5c53aa1f2b63652137fbb2/data/README.md) |
| BOTANIC-1 plant-genome corpus | Public gated release of 7,207,506 redundancy-filtered 8,192-bp windows (59,043,889,152 effective bp) from 320 training species plus four held-out genomes; the eight compressed training shards preserve sequence case, augmentation margins, train/test separation, source manifests, and assembly metadata. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.09.04.749355)<br>🤗 [Pretraining dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/living-models/Botanic1-pretraining) |
| Creator 1.0 phylogeny-guided plant corpus | Whole-genome sequence from 1,000 plant species ordered into 83 consecutive phylogenetic pretraining stages, yielding 52 cumulative checkpoints associated with plant orders. The paper does not link a source manifest or processed corpus release. | 📄 [Preprint](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.10.22.761197) |
| MarinDNA exp586/exp593 PlantCAD training inventory | 2,638,656 original 8,192-bp PlantCAD training windows across 65 species, verified against 44 training metadata shards at dataset revision `4a444fff5520b992aa978d92a5af509a81977098`. exp593 reuses this inventory with reverse-complement, case, metadata and annotation-guided FIM augmentation; annotations cover 23 species. The upstream `valid.tsv` is excluded because it overlaps PlantCAD2 Andropogoneae benchmark windows. Documented and versioned training resource; a standalone download endpoint was not independently verified in this audit. | 💻 [Pinned training and source-verification documentation](https://github.com/Open-Athena/marin-dna/blob/16207a4be2c4b6cb7b3e5d31f59dd6557c06998a/experiments/exp593_augmented_dna/README.md)<br>🤗 [Released model provenance and manifests](https://huggingface.co/marin-dna/marin-dna-exp593/tree/eb26947f8cd596e5bbfd954e434855d5666b030c) |
| MarinDNA m5.1 regional corpus | 443,039,602 reverse-complement-augmented 255-bp sequences across coding, upstream, downstream, enhancer, and ncRNA regions from 499 RefSeq metazoan genomes and 108 Zoonomia mammals; m5.1 saw about 166 billion nucleotide tokens across its staged training lineage. | 🤗 [Dataset collection](https://huggingface.co/collections/marin-dna/a-1b-standard-transformer-rivals-evo-2-40b-on-vep-6a627428aa94a4960c855072)<br>💻 [Data pipeline](https://github.com/Open-Athena/marin-dna/tree/main/snakemake/training_dataset) |
| DNABERT-2 multi-species corpus | Public 135-genome mixture spanning vertebrates, fungi, protozoa, invertebrates, and bacteria (about 32.5 billion A/C/G/T nucleotides), reused by DNAZEN, MergeDNA, and DNAGPT2. | 💻 [Training data](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1dSXJfwGpDSJ59ry9KAp8SugQLK35V83f/view?usp=sharing)<br>💻 [Preparation code](https://github.com/MAGICS-LAB/DNABERT_2) |
| Scorpio Gene-Taxa corpus | Gene-centered bacterial and archaeal corpus with 497 gene types and roughly 2,046 genomes (about 580 million bp), released with held-out taxonomic and gene splits for CARMANIA pretraining and evaluation. | 📄 [Scorpio paper](https://doi.org/10.1038/s42003-025-07902-6)<br>📦 [Zenodo dataset](https://zenodo.org/records/12964684)<br>🤗 [Hugging Face dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/MsAlEhR/scorpio-gene-taxa)<br>💻 [Scorpio code](https://github.com/EESI/Scorpio) |
| Deep-Plant plant functional-genomics corpus | Arabidopsis and rice genome-wide chromatin-state, transcription-factor-binding, histone-modification, gene-expression, and enhancer-activity data processed for supervised sequence-to-function pretraining; the public archive includes about 26.5 GB of data. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.04.06.716755)<br>💻 [Code](https://github.com/Addaoud/DeepPlant)<br>📦 [Zenodo data](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19616358) |
| CodonFM RefSeq CDS corpus | More than 130 million non-viral protein-coding sequences from over 22,000 species (RefSeq 2024-04), filtered into nine taxonomic groups for codon-level masked pretraining. | 📄 [Preprint](https://research.nvidia.com/labs/dbr/assets/data/manuscripts/nv-codonfm-preprint.pdf)<br>💻 [Curation code](https://github.com/NVIDIA-BioNeMo/CodonFM/tree/main/data_scripts) |
| mRNABERT mRNA corpus | Approximately 36 million complete mature mRNA/CDS sequences collected from NCBI nt, MG-RAST, GWH, and MGnify; quality filtering, redundancy reduction, and length filtering yielded about 18 million unique mRNAs for masked pretraining, followed by 500,000 CDS-protein pairs for contrastive alignment. | 📄 [Paper and methods](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41467-025-65340-8)<br>📦 [Zenodo dataset](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.12516160)<br>💻 [Curation code](https://github.com/yyly6/mRNABERT/tree/main/data_process) |
| mRNA-GPT three-domain CDS corpus | Approximately 165 million coding sequences from 19,676 bacterial, 4,688 eukaryotic, and 702 archaeal species (80M, 83M, and 2M sequences, respectively), used to train three separate 302M-parameter generative models. | 📄 [Preprint](https://doi.org/10.64898/2025.12.22.695962)<br>💻 [Code](https://github.com/ZHymLumine/mRNA-GPT)<br>🤗 [Released models](https://huggingface.co/ZYMScott) |
| Sanofi mRNA-GPT full-length corpus | The current preprint reports 30 million natural full-length mRNAs from diverse species and organisms, preserving linked 5′ UTR, CDS, and 3′ UTR regions for end-to-end generative pretraining; no standalone corpus package is currently public. | 📄 [Preprint and data description](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.03.31.715707) |
| NUWA multi-domain coding-sequence corpus | Approximately 115.1 million CDS records spanning bacteria (80M sequences; 19,676 species), eukaryotes (33M; 4,688 species), and archaea (2.1M; 702 species) for curriculum masked pretraining and supervised contrastive learning; the underlying manuscript is withdrawn and should not be cited. | ⚠️ [Withdrawal notice](https://www.biorxiv.org/content/10.1101/2025.11.01.686058v2)<br>💻 [Training and data scripts](https://github.com/zysxmu/NUWA) |
| RiNALMo ncRNA corpus | 36 million deduplicated and MMseqs2-clustered non-coding RNA sequences assembled from RNAcentral 22.0, Rfam 14.9, NCBI nt, Ensembl 109, and Ensembl Genomes 56; the processed mixture is documented but not released as a standalone package. | 📄 [Paper and data availability](https://pmc.ncbi.nlm.nih.gov/articles/PMC12219582/)<br>💻 [Code](https://github.com/lbcb-sci/RiNALMo) |
| RNABERT RNAcentral/Rfam corpus | 76,237 human small non-coding RNAs (20–440 nt) from RNAcentral for masked pretraining, plus Rfam seed alignments for structural-alignment learning; the family-disjoint configuration excludes BRAliBase2.1 benchmark families from the 3,983-family alignment pool. | 📄 [Paper and methods](https://doi.org/10.1093/nargab/lqac012)<br>💻 [Code, model, and data](https://github.com/mana438/RNABERT) |
| AIDO.RNA RNAcentral 24 corpus | Approximately 42 million deduplicated non-coding RNA sequences from RNAcentral release 24, comprising about 30 billion nucleotides and used for single-nucleotide masked pretraining of the 650M- and 1.6B-parameter models; the processed corpus is documented but not released as a standalone package. | 📄 [Paper and methods](https://doi.org/10.1101/2024.11.28.625345)<br>💻 [ModelGenerator](https://github.com/genbio-ai/ModelGenerator) |
| BiRNA-BERT RNAcentral corpus | Approximately 36 million RNAcentral non-coding RNA sequences comprising 26.42 billion nucleotides, used for paired nucleotide- and 4,096-vocabulary BPE masked pretraining with ALiBi positional biases; the processed corpus is documented but not released as a standalone package. | 📄 [Paper and methods](https://doi.org/10.1038/s42003-025-08982-0)<br>💻 [Code](https://github.com/buetnlpbio/BiRNA-BERT) |
| HydraRNA full-length RNA corpus | 28.09 million redundancy-filtered coding and non-coding RNAs (about 51 billion nucleotide tokens) assembled from RNAcentral release 24 and NCBI RefSeq annotations across 1,277 species; sequences longer than 4,096 nt were segmented for pretraining. | 📄 [Paper and methods](https://doi.org/10.1186/s13059-025-03853-7)<br>💻 [Code](https://github.com/GuipengLi/HydraRNA) |
| RIBOSPAN-FM-Corpus | 67,747,085 RNA sequences (about 55 GB) from RNAcentral v26, Ensembl 115, and Ensembl Genomes 62, used for bidirectional pretraining with native contexts up to 10,240 nt. | 📄 [Paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22849)<br>🤗 [Dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/SII-GAIR-NLP/RIBOSPAN-FM-Corpus)<br>💻 [Curation code](https://github.com/GAIR-NLP/RIBOSPAN-FM) |
| LORE multimodal pretraining resource | MIMIC's documented resource combines about 13M RNA transcripts, 15.5M proteins, and 4B+ natural-language tokens from more than 6,000 organisms; the public release currently exposes examples and metadata rather than the complete corpus. | 📄 [Paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.24506)<br>💻 [Code](https://github.com/PolymathicAI/MIMIC)<br>🤗 [LORE examples](https://huggingface.co/datasets/polymathic-ai/LORE-examples) |
| Shorkie Saccharomycetales corpus | 385,551 chromosome-held-out 16,384-bp training windows built from 165 Saccharomycetales genome assemblies (about 3.7 GB including source genomes and TFRecords); accession lists and the build pipeline are public, with packaged downloads mirrored from requester-pays storage. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.1101/2025.09.19.677475)<br>📦 [Data catalogue](https://khchao.com/shorkie/content/data_resources.html)<br>💻 [Build pipeline](https://github.com/calico/shorkie-paper/tree/main/scripts/01_data_build/lm_corpus) |
| OpenRNA v1 | 114,186,538 curated full-length RNA sequences (231.3 billion nt; 46 GB) harmonized from 17 public databases across 15 RNA types and all major domains of life for EVA pretraining. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.03.17.712398)<br>🤗 [Dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/GENTEL-Lab/OpenRNA-v1-114M)<br>💻 [Training code](https://github.com/GENTEL-lab/EVA/tree/main/training) |
| EukaUTR two-stage 3′ UTR corpus | 7,084,544 non-redundant 3′ UTRs from 1,719 species-disjoint eukaryotic genomes: 7,025,323 broad-eukaryotic sequences (3.268 billion nt) followed by 59,221 high-confidence human, mouse, rat, and zebrafish sequences (48.20 million nt). | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.09.07.749809)<br>📦 [Zenodo data](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21790090)<br>💻 [Code](https://github.com/meilanglang/EukaUTR) |
| RNASeek cross-phyla transcriptomic resource | Cross-phyla transcriptomic sequences combined with RNA secondary-structure annotations and natural-language tokens for causal pretraining; the source and workflow are documented, but no separately sized corpus card is released. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.09.24.754173)<br>🤗 [Full release and pretraining artifacts](https://huggingface.co/JoyXiangLab/rnaseek-full)<br>💻 [Code](https://gitlab.com/schen647/rnaseek) |
| RiboGRAM ncRNA corpus | Approximately 40 million non-redundant ncRNA sequences assembled from RNAcentral 25.0, Rfam 15.0, and Ensembl release 115 after MMseqs2 deduplication at 100% identity and 0.8 coverage; the release also includes CDS and mRNA control corpora. | 📄 [Preprint and methods](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.10.12.758266)<br>📦 [Released pretraining data](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.23201637) |
| Eco-RNA staged genomic/RNA mixture | A 300,000-update first stage over four OpenGenome2 genomic buckets, its non-coding-RNA partition, and filtered RNAcentral sequences, followed by 200,000 updates on the same sources with increased RNA sampling; the paper documents the recipe but does not release a corpus package. | 📄 [Preprint and training details](https://arxiv.org/html/2610.13106) |
| LIMBER RNAcentral corpus | 40,498,819 exact-sequence-deduplicated RNAcentral records used for single-nucleotide MLM; sequences up to 1,022 nt are consumed whole and longer RNAs are randomly cropped and resampled each epoch. The processed corpus is not redistributed as a standalone package. | 💻 [Pretraining pipeline and data description](https://github.com/ziyaow1010/LIMBER#pretraining)<br>🌐 [RNAcentral source](https://ftp.ebi.ac.uk/pub/databases/RNAcentral/current_release/sequences/)<br>⚠️ No paper DOI or repository licence is currently declared |
| RNAChat text–RNA training corpus | 1,509,419 natural-language/RNA pairs split into 1,298,904 train, 104,812 validation, and 105,703 test rows. The bilingual templates and selected Qwen2.5 paraphrases condition packed mRNA, miRNA, tRNA, snRNA, snoRNA, rRNA, and other ncRNA sequences on class, family, species, length, composition, and function; splits use class-specific leakage units, including Rfam-family separation for ncRNA. | 🤗 [Public corpus and data card](https://huggingface.co/JWJPKU/RNAChat-corpus)<br>📚 [Source manifest and preparation notes](https://huggingface.co/JWJPKU/RNAChat-corpus/tree/main/docs)<br>⚠️ Published in a model repository with a placeholder `license: other`; upstream terms are source-specific, and the linked RNAChat-v7 model/training code are not public |
| RNAElectra RNAcentral corpus | 44,613,362 valid sequences retained from 45,316,669 active RNAcentral records after removing ambiguous bases and sequences longer than 1,024 nt, then split 90:10 for replaced-token-discriminator pretraining and validation. | 📄 [Preprint and data availability](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.03.15.711950)<br>🤗 [Model card](https://huggingface.co/FreakingPotato/RNAElectra) |
| Uni-RNA multi-source RNA corpus | Approximately one billion coding and non-coding RNA sequences assembled from RNAcentral, NCBI, GWH, MG-RAST, and MGnify for masked-language pretraining of models up to 400M parameters; the processed mixture is documented but not released as a standalone package. | 📄 [Preprint](https://doi.org/10.1101/2023.07.11.548588)<br>💻 [Official release](https://github.com/ComDec/unirna_tf) |
| RNA-BERTa class-balanced RNA corpus | 10,841,246 RNAs (9,757,119 train; 1,084,127 validation; about 1.22 billion tokens) combining SILVA rRNAs, RNAcentral miRNAs/repeats/riboswitches/ribozymes, and NCBI Virus RNAs; BLAST-based filtering removed sequences overlapping the downstream fine-tuning set. | 📄 [Paper and methods](https://doi.org/10.1093/bib/bbag488)<br>🤗 [Model card](https://huggingface.co/IlPakoZ/RNA-BERTa9700) |
| RNAGenesis clustered RNAcentral corpus | Approximately 42 million clustered non-coding RNA sequences from RNAcentral used for hybrid n-gram masked pretraining of the encoder before latent-diffusion training; preparation and training scripts are public. | 📄 [Preprint](https://doi.org/10.1101/2024.12.30.630826)<br>💻 [Data pipeline](https://github.com/zaixizhang/RNAGenesis)<br>📦 [Release](https://zenodo.org/records/15203813) |
| structRFM sequence-structure corpus | About 21 million RNAcentral release 24 sequence–secondary-structure pairs no longer than 512 nt, with structures predicted by BPfold and MUSES for structure-guided masked pretraining; the current pretraining dataset has a dedicated Zenodo archive. | 📄 [Nature paper](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41586-026-11210-2)<br>📦 [Pretraining data](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.18272040)<br>📦 [Archived code](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.18274292)<br>💻 [Code](https://github.com/heqin-zhu/structRFM) |
| UltraGen UltraSelex corpus | Ten million RNA sequences selected at less than 90% identity from the larger UltraSelex experimental pool (more than 40 million sequences) for base-model pretraining; continued-pretraining resources include 12-SELEX, 3′ UTR, and RIP-seq collections in the public archive. | 📄 [Preprint](https://doi.org/10.21203/rs.3.rs-4461517/v2)<br>📦 [Data, code, and models](https://zenodo.org/records/15062262) |
| C2L2 long-read transcriptome corpus | Approximately 35 million PacBio long reads from 26 cancer cell lines spanning nine tissues, used for long-context transcriptome pretraining of Mach-1/LoRNA-SH and for transcript-architecture analyses. | 📄 [Current preprint](https://doi.org/10.1101/2024.08.26.609813)<br>💻 [LoRNA-SH pipeline](https://github.com/fourpartswater/lorna-sh) |
| RNALens two-stage RNA corpus | Multi-species genomic sequences followed by curated 5′ UTRs for domain-adaptive pretraining with masked-token, secondary-structure, and minimum-free-energy objectives; no separately sized corpus package is currently released. | 📄 [Preprint](https://doi.org/10.1101/2025.07.20.665722)<br>🤗 [Model card](https://huggingface.co/oomics/RNALens) |
| CaLM/ENA multi-species CDS corpus (CodonMamba) | 8,771,938 coding sequences from 1,544 organisms across the domains of life, inherited from the CaLM/ENA resource for codon-level bidirectional-Mamba pretraining; the official project documents the preparation workflow. | 📄 [Preprint](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.08.24.746601)<br>💻 [Code and data instructions](https://github.com/meilanglang/CodonMamba)<br>🤗 [Model card](https://huggingface.co/langmei/CodonMamba) |
| RNA-FM RNAcentral100 corpus | 23.7 million non-coding RNA sequences from RNAcentral100 used for single-nucleotide masked pretraining; the processed corpus is documented but not released as a standalone package. | 📄 [Paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2204.00300)<br>💻 [Code and data documentation](https://github.com/ml4bio/RNA-FM) |
| GenerRNA RNAcentral 22 corpus | 16.09 million sequences (17.4 billion nt) retained from 34.39 million RNAcentral release 22 records after length filtering and MMseqs2 clustering at 80% identity; messenger RNAs were excluded while more than 2,600 Rfam families and 30 RNA types were represented. | 📄 [PLOS ONE paper](https://doi.org/10.1371/journal.pone.0310814)<br>💻 [Curation code](https://github.com/pfnet-research/GenerRNA)<br>🤗 [Model and data release](https://huggingface.co/pfnet/GenerRNA) |
| RFamLlama family-conditioned corpus | Approximately 676,000 Rfam sequences from more than 4,000 families, partitioned with family-wise clustering at 90% sequence identity and paired with RNA-family control tags for conditional autoregressive pretraining. | 📄 [Workshop paper](https://openreview.net/forum?id=dXnQedxEJD)<br>💻 [Preparation code](https://github.com/JinyuanSun/RFamLlama)<br>🤗 [Model card](https://huggingface.co/jinyuan22/RFamLlama-base) |
| ChaRNABERT RNAcentral/RefSeq mixture | Roughly 31 million non-coding RNA sequences from RNAcentral and 31 million coding RNA sequences from RefSeq, used in non-coding-only and combined pretraining configurations with learned gradient-based subsequence tokenization; no standalone processed corpus is public. | 📄 [Preprint and data description](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.11808) |
| RNAtomy RNAcentral structural corpus | Documentation reports 200,158 RNAcentral sequences of 50–1,024 nt after category-aware sampling, 95%-identity clustering, and structural-feature filtering, split into 180,142 training and 20,016 validation RNAs. The CC BY 4.0 archive instead documents 246,458 FASTAs, residue descriptors for 114,936 RNAs, and 70 example pairwise maps; the complete approximately 130-GB map collection is request-only, so the public files do not exactly match the stated training split. | 📄 [Preprint](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.10.765792)<br>📦 [Checkpoint and released corpus assets](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.23968854)<br>💻 [Pinned corpus documentation](https://github.com/Azam-Shi/RNAtomy/tree/2c00d5859b4dc2be7f246bb057f23baeb6264c47)<br>⚠️ The documented pretraining entry point is not present in the repository; trRosettaRNA, DSSR, and RNAcentral retain their own terms |
| LAMAR mammalian/viral transcript corpus | 15,000,406 curated genes and transcripts (about 267 billion nucleotides) retained from an initial 21M-sequence RefSeq/RNAcentral/NCBI-genome collection, spanning 225 mammals and 1,569 mammalian viruses. | 📄 [Genome Biology paper](https://doi.org/10.1186/s13059-025-03752-x)<br>💻 [Code](https://github.com/rnasys/LAMAR)<br>🤗 [Model cards](https://huggingface.co/zhw-e8/LAMAR) |
| mRNA-FM RefSeq CDS corpus | 45 million messenger-RNA coding sequences used for codon-level masked pretraining of the 239M-parameter mRNA-FM model; the official implementation identifies the source as RefSeq CDS, but the processed corpus is not released as a standalone package. | 💻 [Official documentation](https://github.com/ml4bio/RNA-FM)<br>📦 [Original March 2024 release](https://github.com/ml4bio/RNA-FM/commit/147dfc4f423f3e5ce21b51dd245841e007efa7ed)<br>🤗 [Checkpoint](https://huggingface.co/cuhkaih/rnafm/blob/main/mRNA-FM_pretrained.pth) |
| OneKP structure-aware RNA corpus (PlantRNA-FM / MP-RNA / OmniGenome) | Approximately 25.0 million RNA sequences (54.2 billion bases) from 1,124 plant species in OneKP. PlantRNA-FM filters sequences to 20–512 nt and adds ViennaRNA structures plus CDS/5′ UTR/3′ UTR labels; MP-RNA and OmniGenome use the same OneKP-scale source with predicted structures for structure-aware curricula and sequence–structure alignment. | 📄 [PlantRNA-FM paper](https://doi.org/10.1101/2024.06.24.600509)<br>📄 [MP-RNA paper](https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.304/)<br>📄 [OmniGenome paper](https://doi.org/10.1609/aaai.v39i20.35500)<br>💻 [Code](https://github.com/yangheng95/OmniGenBench)<br>🌐 [OneKP source](https://db.cngb.org/onekp/) |
| RNAret RNAcentral 21 corpus | Approximately 29.8 million non-coding RNAs from RNAcentral release 21.0 used for 1-, 3-, and 5-mer masked pretraining with sequences up to 2,000 nt. | 📄 [Paper and methods](https://doi.org/10.1038/s42003-026-09757-x)<br>🌐 [RNAcentral release 21](https://ftp.ebi.ac.uk/pub/databases/RNAcentral/releases/21.0/)<br>📦 [Models and data](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.18313475) |
| 3UTRBERT GENCODE human 3′ UTR corpus | Human 3′ UTR sequences derived from 108,573 unique GENCODE release 40 mRNA transcripts, selecting the longest primary transcript per gene and using overlapping 3- to 6-mer masked-language pretraining. | 📄 [Paper and methods](https://doi.org/10.1002/advs.202407013)<br>💻 [Code](https://github.com/yangyn533/3UTRBERT)<br>📦 [Released data](https://doi.org/10.6084/m9.figshare.26082916.v1) |
| RNA-MSM Rfam/RNAcmap3 MSA corpus | RNAcmap3-generated homologous alignments for 3,932 Rfam 14.7 families after excluding families with experimentally determined structures; the median alignment contains 2,184 sequences. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.1093/nar/gkad1031)<br>💻 [Code and MSA workflow](https://github.com/yikunpku/RNA-MSM) |
| RNAErnie RNAcentral corpus | Approximately 23 million RNAcentral sequences with RNA-type labels used for base-, subsequence-, and motif-level masked pretraining of the original RNAErnie model. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.1038/s42256-024-00836-4)<br>💻 [Code and preprocessing](https://github.com/CatIIIIIIII/RNAErnie) |
| ERNIE-RNA RNAcentral 21 corpus | 20.4 million non-coding RNA sequences retained from 34 million RNAcentral release 21 records after vocabulary refinement, exact-duplicate removal, and filtering sequences longer than 1,024 nt. | 📄 [Paper and methods](https://doi.org/10.1038/s41467-025-64972-0)<br>💻 [Code](https://github.com/Bruce-ywj/ERNIE-RNA) |
| UTR-LM five-species 5′ UTR corpus | 214,349 endogenous 5′ UTRs from human, rat, mouse, chicken, and zebrafish in Ensembl, augmented with experimentally measured synthetic/random 5′ UTR libraries for MLM, secondary-structure, and minimum-free-energy objectives. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.1038/s42256-024-00823-9)<br>💻 [Code and data instructions](https://github.com/a96123155/UTR-LM) |
| SpliceBERT 72-vertebrate primary-RNA corpus | More than 2 million non-redundant primary RNA sequences covering approximately 65 billion nucleotides, extracted from UCSC reference genomes and annotations across 72 vertebrates; 50,000 sequences were held out for validation. | 📄 [Paper and methods](https://doi.org/10.1093/bib/bbae163)<br>💻 [Preparation and training code](https://github.com/chenkenbio/SpliceBERT)<br>📦 [Released data](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.7995778) |
| Parnet ENCODE eCLIP corpus | Public 4.95-GB HFDS archive built from 223 ENCODE eCLIP experiments spanning 150 RNA-binding proteins in K562 and HepG2; human gene annotations are segmented into 600-nt tiles with matched eCLIP and size-matched-input profiles for multi-task training. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.08.08.743506)<br>📦 [Zenodo dataset](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.14176118)<br>💻 [Data pipeline](https://github.com/marsico-lab/parnet-marsico-lab) |
| FungiHelixSeek fungal-genome corpus | Fungal genomic sequences used for single-nucleotide masked pretraining; the project documents the source as fungal genomes and provides the reproducible model/data release, but does not currently publish a separate standalone pretraining-corpus package. | 💻 [Project and data docs](https://github.com/zhangtaolab/FungiHelixSeek)<br>🤗 [Model collection](https://huggingface.co/collections/zhangtaolab/fungihelixseek) |
| HViLM VIRION continued-pretraining corpus | Approximately 5 million non-redundant 1,000-nt viral fragments selected after quality control, deduplication, and MMseqs2 clustering at 80% identity/coverage; spans about 9,000 virus species and 45+ families. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.03.18.712700)<br>🤗 [Model card and data description](https://huggingface.co/duttaprat/HViLM-base) |
| FishCaduceus fish-genome corpus | Public single-nucleotide MLM windows from eight fish reference genomes: 6,087,221 512-bp windows plus 2,709,306 1,024-bp windows, covering seven cyprinid aquaculture fishes and zebrafish. | 🤗 [512-bp dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/FishCaduceus/FishCaduceus-Pretraining-512)<br>🤗 [1,024-bp dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/FishCaduceus/FishCaduceus-Pretraining-1024) |
| BatatasLM sweetpotato/Ipomoea corpus | Two public 4,096-bp masked-LM corpora: SP5 contains 425,080 windows from five sweetpotato genomes/accessions, while MS12 contains 482,122 windows from five sweetpotato plus seven related *Ipomoea* genomes. | 💻 [Project](https://github.com/Weiyiliang/BatatasLM)<br>🤗 [SP5 dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/BatatasLM/BatatasLM-SP5-Pretraining-Data)<br>🤗 [MS12 dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/BatatasLM/BatatasLM-MS12-Pretraining-Data) |
| Canadian Invertebrates 1.5M barcode corpus | About 1.5 million 660-bp DNA barcodes from the Barcode of Life Data System, with taxonomy and specimen metadata; used for BarcodeBERT and BarcodeMamba self-supervised pretraining and held-out species evaluation. | 🤗 [Processed dataset](https://huggingface.co/datasets/bioscan-ml/CanadianInvertebrates-ML)<br>📄 [BarcodeMamba paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.11084)<br>💻 [BarcodeBERT pipeline](https://github.com/bioscan-ml/BarcodeBERT) |
| BIOSCAN-5M barcode pretraining corpus | 5.15 million arthropod specimens yielding 2.4 million unique COI barcodes; BarcodeMAE+ pretrains on 2.28 million sequences without species-level labels and uses available genus labels for its auxiliary objective. | 📄 [BarcodeMAE+ paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35877)<br>📄 [BIOSCAN-5M paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.12723)<br>💻 [Dataset repository](https://github.com/bioscan-ml/BIOSCAN-5M)<br>🤗 [Dataset card](https://huggingface.co/datasets/bioscan-ml/BIOSCAN-5M) |
| MycoAI fungal ITS corpus | 5.23 million UNITE+INSD internal-transcribed-spacer sequences representing about 14,700 species across a long-tailed fungal taxonomy; the pretraining split contains mostly unlabeled barcodes for BarcodeMamba+ and BarcodeMAE+ adaptation. | 📄 [BarcodeMamba+ paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.15931)<br>📄 [BarcodeMAE+ paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35877)<br>💻 [Code and data pipeline](https://github.com/bioscan-ml/BarcodeMamba)<br>💻 [MycoAI data source](https://github.com/MycoAI/MycoAI) |
| mtDNA-FM mitochondrial corpus | Two-stage corpus of 117,615 vertebrate mitochondrial genomes from NCBI followed by 34,975 quality-filtered human HmtDB sequences (about 152,000 complete mtDNA genomes), with a heteroplasmy-aware second phase. | 💻 [Project and data pipeline](https://github.com/vthawfeek/mtdna-foundation-model)<br>🤗 [Model card](https://huggingface.co/vthawfeek/mtdna-foundation-model) |
| HaploLM human mitochondrial corpus | 49,310 quality-filtered human mitochondrial genomes from GenBank, drawn from MITOMAP's full-length accession list and used for causal full-sequence training; exact duplicates stay within one split and six complete Mitotree branches are held out. The repository provides accession IDs and deterministic build/split scripts rather than redistributing sequences or Mitotree labels. | 💻 [MIT-licensed data and split pipeline](https://github.com/FaiasPromit/HaploLM)<br>🤗 [Model card and training specification](https://huggingface.co/FaiasPromit/HaploLM)<br>⚠️ GenBank, MITOMAP, HaploGrep, and Mitotree retain their upstream terms |
| eccDNAMamba circular-DNA corpus | CircleBase (601,036 annotated human eccDNAs) and eccDNA Atlas (629,987 animal eccDNAs) supply the circular sequences. The paper reports a filtered 524-million-bp, 101.5-million-BPE-token pretraining mixture, while giving inconsistent sequence totals of 120,000 and 1,087,886; no separate pretraining-corpus package is released. | 📄 [Paper and methods](https://arxiv.org/html/2506.18940)<br>💻 [Preparation and training code](https://github.com/zzq1zh/eccDNAMamba) |
| MicroGlot microbial cross-domain corpus | 3.70 million sequences comprising 378.3 billion nucleotides across 99,700 bacterial, archaeal, fungal, protist, viral, and plasmid species, assembled from NCBI RefSeq release 232, ICTV VMR MSL40 v1, and PLSDB 2024_05_31_v2 with NCBI Taxonomy metadata; the processed corpus is described but not released as a standalone package. | 📄 [Paper](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.09.22.753215)<br>🤗 [Model card and species assets](https://huggingface.co/athanzli/MicroGlot)<br>💻 [Code](https://github.com/athanzli/MicroGlot) |
| PhaGen phage-genome corpus | 98,665 phage genomes totaling 4,800,631,119 bases: 52,836 MGV, 29,768 GPD, and 16,061 NCBI records. The official configuration uses a seed-42 95%/5% Parquet split; training code and source lineage are public, but the complete training FASTA is not redistributed. | 🤗 [Model card and corpus counts](https://huggingface.co/YusenWithR/PhaGen)<br>💻 [Training configuration and split tooling](https://github.com/ai4nucleome/PhaGen/tree/main/training)<br>📦 [Reproducibility archive](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.23477083) |
| CreatiPhage/CPDD phage-genome corpus | A distinct megaDNA-lineage reconstruction with 99,429 source records totaling 4,771,564,509 bp: 16,489 GenBank, 52,872 MGV, and 30,068 GPD records. Autoregressive pretraining uses a 94,469/4,960 train/validation split; CPDD groups exact/reverse-complement duplicates and derives decontaminated validation/test pools, but the complete corpus and source manifest are not redistributed. | [Preprint](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.07.765118)<br>📄 [Technical report and methods](https://github.com/dhnihaoya/CreatiPhage-Diffusion-DNA-Lm/blob/main/paper/CPDD_technical_report.pdf)<br>💻 [Data-preparation code and requirements](https://github.com/dhnihaoya/CreatiPhage-Diffusion-DNA-Lm#data-preparation)<br>⚠️ Source-specific MGV, GPD, and NCBI terms apply |
| LOAM long-read environmental metagenome corpus | 15,640 species-level representative MAGs from the Micro Flora Danica long-read dataset, yielding about 67.5 Gbp of Oxford Nanopore soil, sediment, and water sequence; 62.4 Gbp is used for training with an 8,192-nt context window and fixed train/validation/test splits. | 📄 [LOAM preprint](https://soilytix.com/papers/loam-preprint.pdf)<br>📄 [Research note](https://soilytix.com/blog/introducing-loam-learning-biology-from-long-read-soil-genomes)<br>📄 [Micro Flora Danica resource](https://www.nature.com/articles/s41564-025-02062-z) |
| Mendel human whole-genome variation corpus | Human whole-genome sequences used for variant-centric pretraining from reference-aligned allelic events; the paper describes the resource but does not currently provide a standalone corpus package. | 📄 [Mendel paper](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.09.28.755183) |

</details>

## DNA LLM Suites and Toolkits

<details>
<summary>Show DNA LLM suites and toolkits</summary>

| Suite | Scope | Links |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Sequence Pruning (SP) | Dynamic sequence-level pruning for DNA pretraining, with Apache-2.0 training, fine-tuning, frozen-probing, data-preparation, and analysis code for DNABERT-2, NT, and HyenaDNA. Five settings reuse MSG/GRCh38 and existing GUE, NT-18, Genomic Benchmarks, BEND, and DART-Eval tasks; pretrained checkpoints are announced but not yet linked. | [Paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.10386)<br>[Code](https://github.com/FaiasPromit/pruning-dna-sequence) |
| DNALLM-Suite | Unified APIs for DNA-model loading, fine-tuning, inference, mutagenesis, interpretation, benchmarking, and MCP integration. | 💻 [Code](https://github.com/zhangtaolab/DNALLM)<br>📘 [Documentation](https://zhangtaolab.org/DNALLM/) |
| EvSpark | MIT-licensed speculative-decoding toolkit for frozen Evo2 7B/20B/40B targets, with distilled drafters, state rollback, and exact block verification. Includes a 24-prompt reproduction subset of the reported 48-prompt evaluation; not an independent foundation-model family or biological benchmark. | 📄 [Preprint](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.09.02.749017)<br>💻 [Code](https://github.com/dhnihaoya/EvSpark)<br>🤗 [Drafter checkpoints](https://huggingface.co/dinghhhhhhhhhhhhhhh/EvSpark)<br>📘 [Reproduction guide](https://github.com/dhnihaoya/EvSpark/blob/main/docs/reproduce.md) |

</details>

| ClinVar GLM variant-pathogenicity reduced-run benchmark | 2026.11 | Gene-disjoint pathogenic/benign SNV classification; fine-tuned and frozen DNABERT-2, Nucleotide Transformer v2 and HyenaDNA, plus zero-shot DNABERT-2/NT controls, versus CADD v1.7; bootstrap intervals, DeLong comparisons and per-consequence analysis | 31,000 ClinVar GRCh38 variants released 2026-11-15: 24,000 train / 3,000 validation / 4,000 test; 1,024-bp reference/alternate windows; one seed, fixed learning rate and at most three epochs | 💻 [Pinned MIT pipeline](https://github.com/Yasmin-maker1/glm-variant-pathogenicity/tree/bfd20ddcf99c7c6c713449b9ceaf5802d381e3c3)<br>📊 [Committed reduced-run report](https://github.com/Yasmin-maker1/glm-variant-pathogenicity/blob/bfd20ddcf99c7c6c713449b9ceaf5802d381e3c3/results/report/RESULTS.md)<br>📋 [Reduced-run configuration](https://github.com/Yasmin-maker1/glm-variant-pathogenicity/blob/bfd20ddcf99c7c6c713449b9ceaf5802d381e3c3/configs/colab.yaml)<br>⚠️ The test set is 10.7% pathogenic and strongly consequence-imbalanced. Per-run predictions, processed variants, fine-tuned weights and checkpoints are not committed; five test variants lack the common CADD comparison, zero-shot coverage differs, and the planned full three-seed/learning-rate/long-context study remains pending |
| KronGenome F held-out sequence-to-function evaluation | 2026.11 | Model-card comparison against AlphaGenome v1 on 24 shared functional tracks, 13 DNase biosamples and stranded splice sites; hg38 chr8/chr9 held out, with AlphaGenome fold-specific windows | 131,072 bp and 1,048,576 bp; log1p track Pearson in 128-bp bins; DNase/splice panels use 23 chr8 and 18 chr9 windows | 📊 [Pinned author-reported results and checkpoint](https://huggingface.co/just-dna-seq/KronGenome-F-1Mb/tree/9acfb4dad8c281e8eac65e97f056c1c338463cb1)<br>💻 [Model/training source](https://github.com/antonkulaga/kron-fm/tree/8ae6fe4ab0956cd737c52ad27e013ea430501099)<br>⚠️ One intermediate checkpoint, reusing existing NTv3/ENCODE/GENCODE resources; not a new general-purpose dataset. Complete frozen windows, predictions and replay bundle unverified; no independent numerical reproduction. AlphaGenome v1 scores higher on all six reported comparisons |
| QSSM-DNA pretraining-transfer evaluation | 2026.11 | Frozen terminal-state probing on human non-TATA promoters, Cohn enhancers and Ensembl open chromatin; independent chr9/chr10 promoter holdout; supervision-placement, token-budget, readout, random-register, GRU, k-mer, CNN, shot/noise and register-size controls | 64/128-nt pretraining windows (256-nt ablation); task-specific Genomic Benchmarks inputs; released 251-bp holdout with 764 non-TATA promoters and 641 gene-body negatives in 1,378 overlap clusters | 📦 [Versioned v1.0.0 release](https://github.com/Gumgo91/QSSM-DNA/releases/tag/v1.0.0)<br>📊 [Pinned result tables and analysis scripts](https://github.com/Gumgo91/QSSM-DNA/tree/a3be884b6891b3db8b61006dfbe65c90361e8823/results)<br>📦 [114-MB per-seed prediction archive](https://github.com/Gumgo91/QSSM-DNA/releases/download/v1.0.0/predictions.zip)<br>📋 [Holdout manifest and SHA-256 provenance](https://github.com/Gumgo91/QSSM-DNA/blob/a3be884b6891b3db8b61006dfbe65c90361e8823/data/holdout_promoter_chr9_chr10.manifest.json)<br>⚠️ Ten-seed exact-simulation results reuse three existing Genomic Benchmarks tasks but add the independent holdout. Pretrained checkpoints, run directories and cached features are not released; no DOI/publisher record for the in-press paper was located and no quantum-hardware execution is demonstrated |
| Plant read placement (PlantCaduceus audit) | 2026.11 | Known-species identification, unseen-relative placement and novelty detection; frozen/adapted PlantCaduceus versus canonical 5-mers and full-genome/training-region minimap2 | 80,000 Badread-simulated reads from 28 plant species (13 panel / 6 validation / 9 held-out); mean 8 kb reads, up to eight 512-bp model windows; 1/10/50 reads per specimen | 💻 [Pinned MIT code and configurations](https://github.com/gbadedata/plant-read-placement/tree/342f1abc03d0c0a14db27ccc234c534efff290a5)<br>📊 [Test results](https://github.com/gbadedata/plant-read-placement/blob/342f1abc03d0c0a14db27ccc234c534efff290a5/results/test/RESULTS.md)<br>📋 [Frozen protocol](https://github.com/gbadedata/plant-read-placement/blob/342f1abc03d0c0a14db27ccc234c534efff290a5/PREREG.md)<br>⚠️ Simulated reads only; protocol/configuration hashes verified, numerical reproduction not run. Raw reads, trained heads/adapted weights, calibration files and referenced genome lock are not bundled. Full-genome minimap2 sees test regions; training-region control also reported. No tagged release or standalone manuscript located |
| CoPRA protein–RNA affinity and mutation-effect open replay | 2026.11 | Absolute protein–RNA binding-affinity prediction, ordinary and local mutation-effect prediction, controlled method comparisons, intervention analyses and independent external validation; cross-domain pretrained protein/RNA representations versus task and service baselines | 752 absolute-affinity units across 113 families; 338 ordinary mutation pairs; 220 local pairs across 21 landscapes / 20 families; 4,512 endpoint prediction rows | 📦 [Versioned release v2026.11.17](https://github.com/simshk2023/protein-rna-affinity-audit/releases/tag/v2026.11.17)<br>💻 [MIT replay and released study outputs](https://github.com/simshk2023/protein-rna-affinity-audit/tree/v2026.11.17)<br>📋 [Verification matrix](https://github.com/simshk2023/protein-rna-affinity-audit/blob/v2026.11.17/VERIFICATION_MATRIX.md)<br>⚠️ No public manuscript identifier located. ProNAB observations, independent-validation measurements, third-party model weights, service response bodies and fitted PRA-Pred coefficients are not redistributed; the release supplies acquisition routes, frozen predictions and a 160-check clean-room replay |
| DeepINSIGHT HyenaDNA–LINSIGHT audit | 2026.11 | Tests whether mean HyenaDNA embeddings or TopK sparse-autoencoder codes add to LINSIGHT for non-coding disease-variant ranking; locked HGMD evaluation, held-out INSIGHT likelihood, width-matched random/shuffled controls and power simulations | 1,001-bp sequence windows; 1,564 usable positives in unmatched, TSS-matched and region-matched panels of 161,329 / 16,910 / 6,286 rows; 1,362,112 sampled INSIGHT sites | 💻 [Pinned MIT pipeline and report source](https://github.com/Julien-Mutton/DeepINSIGHT/tree/54b7f3a16ac3380893df70328d9f9842f5575688)<br>📊 [Committed result report](https://github.com/Julien-Mutton/DeepINSIGHT/blob/54b7f3a16ac3380893df70328d9f9842f5575688/results/rate_0.001/REPORT.md)<br>📋 [Input provenance and access limits](https://github.com/Julien-Mutton/DeepINSIGHT/blob/54b7f3a16ac3380893df70328d9f9842f5575688/data/README.md)<br>⚠️ HyenaDNA inputs did not improve the recorded tests. Licensed HGMD pickles and approximately 95 GB of upstream inputs are not bundled; the locked three-seed/10%-holdout HGMD plan differs from current five-seed/50%-holdout defaults, the 1% run is pending, and no archival paper, tag or release was located |

| LAMP artificial-motif-perturbation evaluation | 2026.11 | Diagnostic evaluation of Enformer post-training: artificial motif-disrupting SNV effects, distal enhancer-gene learning and augmentation; transfer to held-out genes and people | Personal-genome sequences with artificial TF-motif perturbations; exact windows, sample counts and frozen splits unverified | 📄 [Preprint](https://doi.org/10.64898/2026.11.13.766567)<br>📋 [Official abstract and metadata](https://api.biorxiv.org/details/biorxiv/10.64898/2026.11.13.766567)<br>⚠️ Paper-described protocol; tool release announced, but public code, frozen data and numerical reproduction not independently verified; full-text XML access returned HTTP 429 |

## Contributing

Contributions are welcome. Please open an issue or pull request if you find:

- a missing DNA, RNA, nucleotide, or regulatory-sequence foundation model;
- a missing benchmark, dataset, or evaluation protocol;
- a corrected paper, code, model, dataset, or project-page link;
- an incorrect tag, corpus category, context length, parameter count, or objective;
- a benchmark result that should be interpreted with stronger leakage, split, or biological-scope caveats.

When adding a resource, please include a source link and a concise, verifiable description.

## Acknowledgements

We thank the researchers and open-source communities whose work has advanced DNA foundation models, genomic language modeling, regulatory sequence modeling, and sequence-to-function prediction. This repository builds upon publicly available papers, codebases, pretrained models, datasets, benchmarks, and tutorials released by the broader computational biology, genomics, and machine learning communities.

We are especially grateful to the authors and maintainers of the DNA, RNA, nucleotide, regulatory-sequence, microbial, viral, plant, and cross-domain genome modeling resources curated in this repository. Their open scientific contributions make it possible to compare model architectures, pretraining corpora, and evaluation protocols across this rapidly evolving field.

We also thank the community members who report missing models, update links, suggest benchmark corrections, and improve taxonomy labels. Feedback, issues, and pull requests are highly appreciated and will help keep this resource accurate, transparent, and useful for future research.

If this repository is useful to your work, please consider starring it to support its continued maintenance.
